--- PHASE: Client WorkerLoop — 单线程任务处理与停止后 drain  [thread: client worker thread (worker_)] ---

--- PHASE: 后台刷新触发 — RequestBackgroundRefresh  [thread: 调用方线程 (QueryAsync / Wait / RegisterRegions) 或 client worker] ---
  * 触发条件查询  @ kv_semantics/src/client/view/view_server.cc:129-146  :: ShouldRefreshView(status) 对 CONNECTION_ERROR/IO_ERROR/TIMEOUT/NOT_FOUND/BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED 返回 true;TaskResult 重载还检查每个 entryStatus。
  * QueryAsync 触发  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:246-249  :: SubmitAsync 返回后 RecordSubmit,IsRefreshNeeded(status) 则 RequestBackgroundRefresh。
  * Wait 触发  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:313-317  :: TASK_NOT_FOUND 直接返回;否则 viewServer_ 非空且 ShouldRefreshView(status) 或 ShouldRefreshView(result) 时 RequestBackgroundRefresh(此处读 viewServer_ 无锁)。
  * RegisterRegions 触发  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:325-327  :: RegisterRegionsOnce 输出 needRefresh(例如 RegisterMemory 失败且 IsRefreshNeeded,360 行)时请求刷新。

  * 回收上一轮刷新线程  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:884  :: 释放 mutex_ 之后 join completedThread(避免持锁 join)。

  * 发布前二次校验、retire 与替换  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:846-860  :: 持 mutex_;再次检查 initialized_/refreshInProgress_ 与 ShouldPublishView(848-851,防止并发期间已有更新视图);对 oldSnapshot 中不在 nextSnapshot 的 transport 追加到 retiredTransports_(852-857,不调用 Shutdown);snapshot_ = move(nextSnapshot)(859)。

--- PHASE: Shutdown — 摘除状态、drain 与释放资源  [thread: caller thread] ---

--- PHASE: AsuTransport::Shutdown — 每个 transport 的收尾  [thread: caller thread (串行遍历 transports)] ---
  * 停止 transport worker 并 join  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:171-177  :: 持 transport 自己的 producerMu_ 置 stopWorker_=true,notify,join worker_(ConsumerLoop 退出)。
  * 可选等待 in-flight 任务  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:179-191  :: 若 completionWorker_ 可 join 且 config_.timeoutMs != 0,扫描 taskManager_ 存在 INFLIGHT 则 sleep timeoutMs;因此 client Shutdown 延迟可能被每个 transport 放大。
  * 停止 completion worker  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:193-194  :: stopCompletionWorker_=true 并 join completionWorker_。
  * 取消/通知/清理剩余 transport 任务  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:196-203  :: 对所有 task 调 taskExecutor_->Cancel,成功者 NotifyCompletion;随后逐个 taskManager_.Remove。
  * 关闭 executor 与连接管理  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:204-217  :: taskExecutor_->Shutdown() 失败则保留对象并记 finalStatus,成功则 reset;connManager_->Shutdown() 后 reset。

  ? RefreshView 中 viewServer_ == nullptr -> 返回 NOT_INITIALIZED("view server is not initialized")  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:827-829
  ? snapshot_ 非空且 ShouldPublishView(published, fetched) == false -> 返回 OK,不 BuildSnapshot、不发布;判据为两者 viewEpoch 均非 0 且 fetched.viewEpoch <= published.viewEpoch  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:837-839,849-851; kv_semantics/src/client/view/view_server.cc:122-127
  ? 任一侧 viewEpoch == 0(配置型 View 未提供 epoch) -> ShouldPublishView 恒为 true,每次刷新都会重建并发布快照  @ kv_semantics/src/client/view/view_server.cc:122-127
  ? RefreshView 中 BuildSnapshot 失败 -> 返回错误(后台线程只记 WARN);旧 snapshot_ 保持发布;本次新建的 transport 随 nextSnapshot 析构而 Shutdown,但 737 行追加的 providerMemoryStates_ 不会回滚  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:842-844,737,873-881
  ? 发布新快照时某旧 transport 的 nodeId 不在 nextSnapshot -> push_back 到 retiredTransports_,当下不 Shutdown,等 client Shutdown 时统一关闭  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:852-857;226-230
  ? status 属于 CONNECTION_ERROR/IO_ERROR/TIMEOUT/NOT_FOUND/BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED(或 TaskResult 内任一 entry 属于) -> IsRefreshNeeded 为真 -> RequestBackgroundRefresh  @ kv_semantics/src/client/view/view_server.cc:129-146; kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:915-918
  ? Wait 的返回码为 TASK_NOT_FOUND -> 直接 return,跳过 viewServer_ 刷新判断  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:313-317
  ? SubmitAsync 在 producerMu_ 内发现 stopWorker_ 已为 true(与 Shutdown 竞争失败) -> taskManager_.Remove(taskId),taskId=kInvalidTaskId,返回 NotInitialized;反之先入队,由 worker 停止后的 TryPop drain(571-572)处理  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:471-487,571-572
  ? Shutdown 在未 Init 或重复调用时执行 -> initialized_ 已 false、stopWorker_ 已 true;join/移动均为空;Drain 遍历空表返回 OK,函数近似幂等  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:185-241
  ? Shutdown 的 taskManager_.Drain 超时 -> finalStatus 记录 drain 错误,但进程继续 ShutdownSnapshotTransports、retired Shutdown 与 provider unregister(可能仍有 in-flight IO)  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:219-240
  ? ShutdownSnapshotTransports 收到空 snapshot -> 返回 OK,不遍历  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:897-899
  ? 某个 transport->Shutdown() 返回非 OK -> 仅当 finalStatus 仍 OK 时记录该错误,其余 transport 继续关闭  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:222-230,900-904

ERROR PATHS:
  ! 配置文件打开失败: LoadKvClientConfig 返回 NOT_FOUND,消息含 path — kv_semantics/src/client/utils/config_utils.cc:128-131

  ! 共享/独立 provider 绑定内存失败: 先 nextTransport->Shutdown(),再返回带 nodeId 的错误 — kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:723-731
  ! BindProviderRegions 内部:BindMemory 失败或数量不匹配、绑定后 tokenId 不匹配,先 UnregisterProviderRegions 再返回 — kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:742-784
  ! Router 配置解析失败: BuildSnapshot 返回错误(transports 已建,由上层回滚/析构) — kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:656-661

  ! Shutdown 的 Drain 超时或失败: 记入 finalStatus 但继续释放流程 — kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:219-221

  ! transport 自身 Shutdown: executor/connManager 失败只记录 finalStatus,对象可能不被 reset — kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:204-217

GOTCHAS:
  # Init 在无 mutex_ 的情况下写 config_(135)、viewServer_/transportConfigs_(136-143)、snapshot_/initialized_(180-181);而 GetSnapshot(908-913)在 mutex_ 下读,IsRefreshNeeded(915-918)和 Wait(313-317)直接无锁读 viewServer_。因此 Init 与任何并发调用之间的正确性依赖外部"先 Init 后使用"的串行化,并发 Init/Shutdown/Submit 属数据竞争。
  # worker 线程(179)比 snapshot 发布(180)与 initialized_=true(181)早一行启动;虽然 SubmitAsync 在 initialized_ 为 false 时会被 GetSnapshot 拒绝,但 worker 已开始运行,说明初始化把"线程可见"与"任务可见"分成了两个阶段。
  # taskQueue_.Setup(queueDepth+1) 且 queueDepth=max(2,maxInflightTasks)(148-149,177);SPSC 环空一格(spsc_ring_queue.h:73-82),有效容量正好是 maxInflightTasks。
  # INDEPENDENT 模式在 Init 阶段创建 1 个业务内存 provider + 每 ASU 1 个 transport provider(156,690-702);SHARED 模式只创建 1 个(687-689,714-717),测试分别在 kv_client_impl_test.cc:588-612 验证。
  # Init 时 registeredRegions_ 为空,因此 719-731 的 BindProviderRegions 实际跳过,每个 ProviderMemoryState 初始 regionHandles 为空;绑定只发生在后续 RegisterRegions(330-426)或刷新时新建 transport(674-740)。
  # 后台刷新从不在发布时关闭被移除的 transport,只放入 retiredTransports_(852-857);唯一关闭点是 Shutdown(207,226-230)。长时间运行且视图频繁变更会累积旧 transport 的连接/线程/缓冲直到进程退出。
  # RefreshView 失败路径不回收 BuildTransport 追加到 providerMemoryStates_ 的条目(kv_client_impl.cc:737 与 842-844);只有 Init 失败路径(170-175)会整体清空。刷新失败会留下"无 transport 但仍被跟踪"的 provider 状态直到 Shutdown。
  # RefreshView 的守卫是 (!initialized_ && !refreshInProgress_)(823/836/848),而不是单纯 !initialized_:Shutdown 先把 initialized_ 置 false(189)但仍 join 刷新线程(198),所以已在途的刷新仍可完成发布;Shutdown 的 join 在移动 snapshot_(204-211)之前,能捕获这次发布。
  # Shutdown 顺序是 JoinBackgroundRefresh(198)-> worker join(199),而 client worker 在 567 行可能请求刷新;因为 initialized_ 已被 189 行置 false,RequestBackgroundRefresh 在 870 行直接返回,不会留下未被 join 的刷新线程。
  # Shutdown 在 Drain 超时后仍继续 ShutdownSnapshotTransports(222-225)并 unregister provider 内存(231-240);若仍有 in-flight transport IO,Drain 只保证等待到超时,存在 transport 被 shutdown 后任务仍引用其资源、以及 provider 内存被 unregister 后仍被使用的风险。
  # Drain 的超时是逐任务计算的(task_manager.cc:118-132 对每个未完成任务调用 WaitContext 366-384),因此最坏总等待约为"未完成任务数 × defaultWaitTimeoutMs",不是单个全局超时。
  # BuildSnapshot 的复用键只有 nodeId(638-641);同一 nodeId 在新 View 中 endpoint 变化不会重建 transport(ApplyAsuInfoToTransportConfig 只在新建时调用,683),即视图中的地址变更对已存在 transport 不生效。
  # ShouldPublishView 在任一侧 viewEpoch==0 时恒 true(view_server.cc:122-127);ConfigBackedViewServer(101-105)与不带 viewEpoch 的配置文件视图会在每次刷新时都"发布"(但仍复用 transport),刷新去重只在双方 epoch 均已知时生效。
  # Shutdown 读取 defaultWaitTimeoutMs 的时机是 config_ 被重置(208)之前的 188-192 行;若从未 Init,config_ 为默认值,defaultWaitTimeoutMs=100ms(kv_types.h:208-219)。
  # Init 失败不会清理 viewServer_/transportConfigs_/config_(136-143,135),也不会 reset taskQueue_ 之外的状态;失败后重新 Init 会覆盖这些成员,但中间状态不是"干净未初始化"。
  # kMaxShutdownDrainAttempts 声明于 kv_client_impl.cc:102 但全仓库无引用;Shutdown 实际只做一次 taskManager_.Drain(219-221),没有重试语义。
  # ShutdownSnapshotTransports 与 retired transports 的关闭顺序依赖 unordered_map/vector 遍历(901,226),transport 之间的关停顺序不确定;单个 transport 的 Shutdown 若存在 INFLIGHT 任务且 timeoutMs != 0,会 sleep timeoutMs(kv_transport_impl.cc:179-191),可能显著拉长 client Shutdown。
  # BuildTransport 全程持有 memoryMu_(685 到返回),包括 transport Init(与连接建立)与 BindMemory;RegisterRegionsOnce(341-425)也持同一把锁,因此注册内存与新建 transport 完全串行,是初始化/刷新路径的主要锁竞争点。

==========================================================================================
TRACE 1: KvClient::QueryAsync(keys) + Check/Wait for AsuOpType::QUERY end-to-end: client facade -> ClientTask -> Router -> AsuTransport Submit -> Execute (Exist SQE build + send) -> Complet
==========================================================================================
ENTRYPOINTS: kv_semantics/include/kv_client.h:41 KvClient::QueryAsync(const std::vector<CacheKey>&, TaskId&) | kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:244 KvClientImpl::QueryAsync | kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:299 KvClientImpl::Check(TaskId) | kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:301 KvClientImpl::Wait(TaskId, timeoutMs, TaskResult&) | kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport.h:59 AsuTransport::Submit(TransportTaskPtr) | ucm/store/asu/cc/asu_store.cc:758-773 AsuStore Query(): QueryAsync + Wait(queryTimeoutMs) (real UCM caller) | kv_semantics/kv_test/src/kv_client_runner.cc:293-311 Exist(): QueryAsync + Wait

--- PHASE: 1. Public query call, metrics and keys-path submit validation  [thread: caller thread (AsuStore::Query / test runner / user)] ---
  * QueryAsync delegates to the keys overload of SubmitAsync with AsuOpType::QUERY, then records metrics and possibly starts a view refresh  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:244-250  :: status = SubmitAsync(AsuOpType::QUERY, keys, taskId); RecordSubmit(AsuOpType::QUERY, keys.size(), status); if IsRefreshNeeded(status) RequestBackgroundRefresh(). Query metric ids defined at 53-57.
  * Take the immutable view snapshot  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:496-501  :: taskStart = steady_clock::now(); snapshot = GetSnapshot(). If !snapshot || !snapshot->router || snapshot->transports.empty(): taskId = kInvalidTaskId and return NOT_INITIALIZED "client has no ASU transports".
  * Reject non-keys opcodes  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:503-507  :: opType must be QUERY or DELETE, otherwise INVALID_ARGUMENT "keys submit only supports query/delete" and taskId = kInvalidTaskId.
  * Construct ClientTask and size per-key result vector  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:509-515  :: make_unique<ClientTask>; submittedAt=taskStart; opType=QUERY; viewSnapshot=snapshot; keys copied; entryStatus.assign(keys.size(), Status::OK()); queryResult.exists.assign(opType==QUERY ? keys.size() : 0, 0) -> one zero byte per input key, so the final exists vector is already in caller key order and pre-sized. prerequisiteEventHandle stays 0 (never set on this overload).
  * Register the task and obtain its TaskId  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:517-518  :: taskManager_.Submit(std::move(ctx), taskId); on failure return that status unchanged.
  * Re-fetch the shared task context  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:520-524  :: rawCtx = taskManager_.Get(taskId). If null: taskId = kInvalidTaskId, INTERNAL_ERROR "client task disappeared after submit".
  * Serialize producer acceptance against shutdown, stamp enqueue time, push to SPSC queue  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:527-541  :: lock producerMu_; if stopWorker_ -> Remove(taskId), taskId=kInvalidTaskId, NotInitialized(); else enqueuedAt=now, rawCtx->enqueuedAt=enqueuedAt, taskQueue_.TryPush(std::move(rawCtx)). Push failure -> Remove(taskId), taskId=kInvalidTaskId, RESOURCE_BUSY "client task queue is full".
  * Wake the client worker and record enqueue latency  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:542-545  :: workerCv_.notify_one(); metrics kv_client_task_enqueue_duration_seconds = enqueuedAt - taskStart; return OK.

--- PHASE: 2. ClientTaskManager registration state  [thread: caller thread (inside SubmitAsync)] ---
  * Allocate a non-zero, collision-free TaskId and store the shared context as PENDING  @ kv_semantics/src/common/utils/task_manager_base.h:55-73  :: state.store(PENDING); nextTaskId_.fetch_add until id != kInvalidTaskId and not already in tasks_; sharedCtx->taskId = taskId; tasks_.emplace(taskId, sharedCtx) under unique_lock(shared_mutex).

--- PHASE: 3. Client WorkerLoop dequeue and prerequisite-event gate  [thread: single client worker thread (KvClientImpl::WorkerLoop)] ---
  * Set up the runtime device once for the whole worker  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:548-552  :: runtime::Device device; deviceId = config_.transportConfigs.front().deviceId; deviceStatus = deviceId >= 0 ? device.Setup(deviceId) : OK.
  * Per task: stamp processing start and record queue latency  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:553-557  :: ctx->processingStartedAt = now; metric kv_client_task_queue_duration_seconds = processingStartedAt - enqueuedAt.
  * Conditional prerequisite event synchronization  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:558-561  :: status starts as deviceStatus; only if status.ok() AND ctx->prerequisiteEventHandle != 0 is runtime::SynchronizeEvent(handle) called. QUERY never sets the handle, so no event sync happens for query.
  * Process the task or fail it wholesale  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:562-566  :: status.ok() -> taskManager_.Process(ctx); otherwise ClientTaskManager::CompleteWithError(ctx, status).
  * Refresh view on refresh-worthy status, consume queue until stop  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:567-572  :: IsRefreshNeeded(status) -> RequestBackgroundRefresh(); taskQueue_.ConsumerLoop(stopWorker_, producerMu_, workerCv_, processTask); after the loop drain remaining tasks with TryPop.

--- PHASE: 4. ClientTaskManager::Process -> BuildTransportTasks -> Router::RouteKeys on key bytes  [thread: client worker thread] ---
  * Mark INFLIGHT and build per-ASU transport tasks  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:134-145  :: state.store(INFLIGHT); BuildTransportTasks(task); on failure CompleteWithError(task,status) and return.
  * Re-validate the captured snapshot  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:266-271  :: task->viewSnapshot must exist with router and non-empty transports, else NOT_INITIALIZED "client has no ASU transports".
  * Convert CacheKey bytes to router keys and route  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:273-275  :: QUERY/DELETE -> snapshot->router->RouteKeys(ToRouterKeys(task->keys)). ToRouterKeys (52-58) builds std::string from 8 raw key bytes via CacheKeyView. Route result is unordered_map<NodeId, vector<EntryIndex>> of input indices.
  * Verify every routed node has a transport in this snapshot  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:276-282  :: For each routed nodeId, snapshot->transports.find(nodeId) must exist; otherwise NOT_FOUND "routed asu transport not found" with nodeId context (whole client task fails before dispatch).
  * Create one TransportTask per routed node and preserve caller key order  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:284-304  :: nodeId, transport shared_ptr, keys moved by index, originalIndices records the caller index for each moved key.
  * Clear the client's key vector and initialize completion counters  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:305-308  :: task->keys is swapped empty after the move; remainingTransportTasks and remainingTransportSendTasks are set to transportTasks.size().
  * Dispatch to each transport  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:146-153  :: DispatchTask(task); Process returns dispatchStatus and records kv_client_task_process_duration_seconds only when dispatch succeeded.

--- PHASE: 5. DispatchTask -> AsuTransport::Submit -> transport execute queue  [thread: client worker thread] ---
  * Fast-finalize when routing produced no transport tasks  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:312-318  :: Empty transportTasks (e.g. empty keys route to nothing) -> lock waitMu, Finalize(task) immediately with no IO; returns OK.
  * Wire completion/send callbacks and op type, then submit  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:320-341  :: onComplete captures weak_ptr<ClientTask> + transportTaskIndex and calls CompleteTransportTask; onSendComplete decrements remainingTransportSendTasks and records kv_client_task_send_duration_seconds when it reaches 0; transportTask->opType = QUERY; transport->Submit(transportTask).
  * Handle dispatch failure for this and later transport tasks  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:342-347  :: AddContext status with nodeId, CompleteUndispatchedTransportTasks(task, taskIndex, dispatchStatus) (207-228): index==first gets dispatchStatus, later ones get CANCELED, their entryStatus filled, counters decremented, Finalize if all done; dispatch loop returns that status.
  * Transport Submit initializes per-key status and deadline  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:231-238  :: null task -> INVALID_ARGUMENT; entryCount = keys.size() for key ops; task->entryStatus.assign(entryCount, OK); task->deadline = TaskDeadline(config_.timeoutMs) (0 means time_point::max); SubmitTask(task).
  * Transport task registration and SPSC push  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:250-276  :: lock producerMu_; if stopWorker_ or worker not joinable -> NOT_INITIALIZED "transport is not accepting tasks"; taskManager_.Submit(task, taskId); Get(taskId) null -> INTERNAL_ERROR; executeQueue_.TryPush -> RESOURCE_BUSY "transport task queue is full"; notify.
  * Transport WorkerLoop executes the task  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:278-284  :: ConsumerLoop pops and calls taskExecutor_->Execute(task); if Execute returns done (already completed, e.g. pre-send failure) NotifyCompletion is invoked from this worker.

--- PHASE: 6. Execute: IoScheduler split by queryIoNum -> Exist SQE build (sc/seek control) -> Send  [thread: per-ASU transport worker thread (AsuTransportImpl::WorkerLoop)] ---
  * CAS PENDING->INFLIGHT and prepare sub-batches  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:307-320  :: compare_exchange PENDING->INFLIGHT else return false; asuCompletionMetric set; PrepareTaskSubBatches; on success AssignSubBatchConnections.
  * Key-batch branch: reject empty keys, split by QUERY io budget  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:80-99  :: IsKeyBatchOp(QUERY) -> keys.empty() gives INVALID_ARGUMENT "key batch is empty"; else BatchView<CacheKey> and ioScheduler_.SplitForAsu(keys, QUERY), one SubmitKeySubBatchRequest per sub-batch.
  * Determine sub-batch size  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/io_scheduler.cc:80-90  :: GetSqeIoNum(QUERY) returns queryIoNum_ (default 256, kv_types.h:199), already capped at transport Init by server capability capabilities.queryKeys via applyCapLimit (kv_transport_impl.cc:130-138). SplitBatchView (36-52) chunks the key array into consecutive slices of that size.
  * Build the Exist sub-batch request context  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:376-392  :: entryStatus.assign(keys.size(), OK); opcode=ToKvOpcode(QUERY)=KvOpcode::Exist (trans_task_executor.h:41-53); PrepareSubBatchRequest(opType, opcode, AllocateRequestCid(), keys.size(), flagBufferManager_, ctx); BuildSqeRequest -> BuildExistRequest; PackSubBatchRequest.
  * Allocate the flag (completion/result) buffer for Exist  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:125-137  :: PrepareSubBatchRequest sets opType/cid then AllocateSubBatchFlagBuffer. GetFlagBufferSize(Exist) = 16 + (batchNum+7)/8 bytes (72-81): 16-byte CQE header plus 1 bit per key; allocation failure marks the sub-batch completed with the error.
  * Build Exist request fields including sc  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:282-296  :: cid, kv_ns_id from attrs, response_buffer_addr=flagBuffer.device_addr, response_mr_key=flagBuffer.tokenId, rflag=true, sc=GetTransportConfigAttr<bool>(attrs,"sc",true) (default TRUE when attr absent), keys copied, batch_number=keys.size().
  * Capture the seek-control mode on the sub-batch context  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:334-339  :: KvOpcode::Exist case sets subBatchContext.useSeekControl = request.sc before handing the request to the packer. This flag decides which CQE field is consumed at completion.
  * Pack the SQE into the send buffer  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:139-167  :: GetPackedSize(Exist) = (16 + 4*N)*4 bytes (kv_protocol.cc:976-980); sendBufferManager_.Allocate; protocolManager.PackRequest -> KvExistProtocol::PackSqe; any failure calls SetSubBatchBuildFailed (state COMPLETED, status and entryStatus set) and returns the error.
  * Encode SQE dwords  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:982-1003  :: target[0]=(cid<<16)|(fixed bits<<14)|Exist; rflag -> bit13; target[1]=kv_ns_id; target[3..4]=response_buffer_addr (low/high); target[5]=response_mr_key; target[8]=batch_number*16; target[9]=DptrType::Batch<<24; target[10]=batch_number and sc -> bit16. Keys packed 16 bytes each starting at dword 16.
  * Validate the packed request  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:1005-1046  :: batch_number in [1,256] (kMaxExistBatchNumber); batch_number==keys.size(); rflag requires nonzero response addr and mr key; no all-zero key. Violations return INVALID_ARGUMENT, so the sub-batch fails to build.
  * Assign a connection channel to every sub-batch  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:286-305  :: connManager_->SelectConnection() per sub-batch; no channel -> metric connection_errors, entryStatus filled CONNECTION_ERROR, sub-batch COMPLETED, abort.
  * Build send batches and call provider Send  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:115-167  :: BuildSubBatchSendBuffers creates SendIoBatch{connection, sendSge.device_addr, flagBuffer.device_addr, sendSge.length}; SendSubBatchBuffers calls transProvider_->Send(ioBatches, kernel_count, quiet_count); wrong status count -> INTERNAL_ERROR and all sub-batches failed; per-batch failure -> SetSubBatchSendFailed, ReportFailure, release resources.
  * Publish sub-batches and attempt early finalization  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:328-375  :: On success record pre-send/send metrics, sendCompletedAt, sendReturned=true, invoke onSendComplete. Then under task->mutex: if canceled release and return false; on pre-send failure AbortSubBatchesBeforeSend; store subBatchContexts; InitializeRemainingSubBatchCount (counts PENDING only); TryFinalizeFromSubBatches; return Done().

--- PHASE: 7. CompletionLoop Poll -> PollResponseCid/UnpackResponse -> FillEntryStatusFromCqeResult -> CompleteSubBatch  [thread: per-ASU completion worker thread (AsuTransportImpl::CompletionLoop)] ---
  * Snapshot all transport tasks and poll each  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:286-313  :: GetAll(); empty -> sleep 50us; for each ctx taskExecutor_->Poll(ctx) true -> taskManager_.NotifyCompletion(ctx); yield after completionPollSpinLimit idle rounds.
  * Poll guards and deadline check  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:379-405  :: lock task->mutex; return false unless state==INFLIGHT and subBatchContexts non-empty. If now >= deadline: metric completion_timeouts, TIMEOUT status filled into entryStatus, every PENDING sub-batch ReportFailure + CompleteSubBatch(timeout), finalStatus=TIMEOUT, state COMPLETED.
  * Read the completion flag buffer and match cid  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:407-425  :: responseData = subBatchContext.flagBuffer.local_addr. PollResponseCid (kv_protocol.cc:1239-1244) reads data[3]&0xFFFF under acquire; it always returns OK. cid==0 or cid != subBatchContext.cid -> continue (still in flight).
  * Unpack the Exist CQE  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:427-435  :: batchNumber = subBatchContext.entryStatus.size(); ProtocolManager::UnpackResponse(data, ToKvOpcode(QUERY)=Exist, batchNumber, response) (kv_protocol.cc:1228-1237). Failure -> completeWithError(status).
  * UnpackCqe: cid/status, existing_key_number, per-key 1-bit result buffer  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:1053-1060  :: UnpackCqeBase sets out.cid=data[3]&0xFFFF and out.status=(data[3]>>17)&0x7FFF (30-34); out.existing_key_number = data[0] & 0xFFFF; UnpackResultBuffer1Bit(data+4, batch_number, result_buffer) extracts one bit per key (47-56).
  * Map raw CQE status to Status  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:104-137  :: raw 0 -> OK; 0x732 -> CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER ("Batched Result, check result buffer for entry errors"); other known codes (0x701, 0x712, 0x716, 0x723, 0x731, ...) map to their CQE_* codes; unknown -> IO_ERROR.
  * Fill per-key entry statuses from the CQE  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:9-14  :: Wrapper calls FillEntryStatusFromCqeResult(response, subBatchContext.opType, subBatchContext.useSeekControl, subBatchContext.status, subBatchContext.entryStatus). Note it runs with the raw (pre-normalization) sub-batch status.
  * FillEntryStatusFromCqeResult decision tree (sc semantics)  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:139-167  :: status.ok(): all entries ENTRY_KEY_EXIST for QUERY (147). Query with useSeekControl==false and CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER: ignore result_buffer, take existing_key_number = min(entryStatus.size(), response.existing_key_number), fill all ENTRY_KEY_NOT_EXIST then the first N as ENTRY_KEY_EXIST (151-157). Any other non-OK status, or empty result_buffer: fill all entries with that status (159-162). Otherwise (query, sc==true/useSeekControl, CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER, result_buffer present): per entry ResultBufferEntryToStatus(QUERY, bit) -> bit 1 ENTRY_KEY_EXIST, bit 0 ENTRY_KEY_NOT_EXIST (164-166, 87-97).
  * Treat query result-buffer CQE as sub-batch success  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:437-445  :: queryResultBufferStatus = opType==QUERY && subBatchContext.status.code==CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER; effective status = OK for that case, otherwise the mapped status.
  * Report channel health and complete the sub-batch  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:446-456  :: CQE_INTERNAL_ERROR/CQE_IO_TIMEOUT -> connManager_->ReportFailure (io_timeouts metric for timeout), else ReportSuccess; CompleteSubBatch(task, ctx, status) (248-256) releases send/flag slots and channel, sets state COMPLETED, status=effective status, --remainingSubBatchCount.
  * Finalize the transport task when all sub-batches are done  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:51-63  :: TryFinalizeFromSubBatches: empty list -> PARTIAL_FAILED; remainingSubBatchCount != 0 -> return; else finalStatus=BuildFinalStatus (32-41: any sub-batch status non-OK -> PARTIAL_FAILED, else OK); state COMPLETED.
  * Return done and notify from the completion loop  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:457-461  :: TryFinalizeFromSubBatches then done=task->Done(); CompletionLoop calls NotifyCompletion when true.

--- PHASE: 8. NotifyCompletion -> client CompleteTransportTask merge -> Finalize  [thread: transport completion worker, then inline on the same thread in the client callback] ---
  * One-shot transport completion notification  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:65-84  :: Metrics e2e/completion durations; BuildResult(task,result); completionNotified.exchange guard then task->onComplete(result); Remove(transport task id).
  * Transport-level result assembly  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:86-104  :: result.status=finalStatus; entryStatus copied then overwritten in sub-batch order from each subBatchContext.entryStatus; for QUERY result.queryResult=BuildQueryResultFromEntryStatus(result.entryStatus) (bit = code==ENTRY_KEY_EXIST, prefix count computed, trans_task_utils.cc:169-180).
  * Client callback invokes CompleteTransportTask  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:324-329  :: Lambda locks the weak_ptr; if the client task is already gone (e.g. Wait timed out and removed it) the completion is dropped.
  * Lock and de-duplicate the transport-task completion  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:171-175  :: lock task->waitMu; if transportTask->clientCompleted return; completionStatus = result.status.
  * Validate the query result and merge exists by originalIndices  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:177-192  :: QUERY and completionStatus.ok() requires result.queryResult.has_value() else INTERNAL_ERROR "transport query result is missing"; requires exists.size()==transportTask->originalIndices.size() else INTERNAL_ERROR "transport query result size mismatch"; otherwise task->queryResult.exists[originalIndices[i]] = result.queryResult->exists[i], restoring caller key order. invalidQueryResult suppresses the per-entry merge.
  * Merge per-key entry statuses back to caller indices  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:194-200  :: For each i: entryStatus[originalIndices[i]] = (valid && i < result.entryStatus.size()) ? result.entryStatus[i] : completionStatus. Missing keys therefore remain ENTRY_KEY_NOT_EXIST at the client level.
  * Last transport task finalizes the client task  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:202-204  :: remainingTransportTasks.fetch_sub(1)==1 -> Finalize(task).
  * Finalize: prefixHitKeys, aggregate status, wake waiters  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:230-264  :: QUERY: prefixHitKeys re-counted from the merged caller-ordered exists by scanning until the first 0 (232-238), discarding the transport-level prefix count. Any transport task with non-OK finalStatus -> finalStatus=PARTIAL_FAILED, else OK (240-256). Record completion metric, state COMPLETED, cv.notify_all.

--- PHASE: 9. Check/Wait delivery, TaskResult build, task removal  [thread: caller thread] ---
  * Check is a non-blocking lookup  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:102-106  :: Check(taskId) = !task || task->Done(); a missing task (already Waited/removed) reports true.
  * Wait resolves the default timeout and delegates  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:301-304  :: waitMs = timeoutMs==0 ? config_.defaultWaitTimeoutMs : timeoutMs; taskManager_.Wait(taskId, waitMs, result).
  * Lookup and remove the client task  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:108-116  :: Get(taskId) null -> TASK_NOT_FOUND "task not found"; else WaitContext then Remove(taskId) unconditionally (even on timeout).
  * Block on the task condition variable and build the result  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:366-384  :: unique_lock(waitMu); cv.wait_for(waitMs, predicate Done()); BuildResult; if not done override result.status=TIMEOUT with client_task_id/op/wait_ms and log.
  * BuildResult shape  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:352-364  :: result.status = Done()? finalStatus : IN_PROGRESS; result.entryStatus = task->entryStatus; for QUERY result.queryResult = task->queryResult (exists + prefixHitKeys), else queryResult.reset().
  * Post-wait metrics and view refresh  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:305-318  :: Updates kv_client_wait_requests_total/errors_total (error when status or result.status non-OK); returns immediately on TASK_NOT_FOUND; otherwise if viewServer_->ShouldRefreshView(status) or (result) triggers RequestBackgroundRefresh.

BRANCHES:
  ? QueryAsync submit status is refresh-worthy (CONNECTION_ERROR, IO_ERROR, TIMEOUT, NOT_FOUND, BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED) -> RequestBackgroundRefresh() starts a non-blocking view refresh  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:244-250
  ? snapshot null, router null, or transports empty -> taskId = kInvalidTaskId, NOT_INITIALIZED before any task object is created  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:496-501
  ? opType is not QUERY and not DELETE -> INVALID_ARGUMENT "keys submit only supports query/delete"  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:503-507
  ? opType == QUERY when sizing the result -> queryResult.exists is assigned keys.size() zero bytes; for DELETE it stays empty  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:515
  ? stopWorker_ already set (shutdown raced the submit) -> task removed from ClientTaskManager, taskId invalidated, NotInitialized returned  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:528-533
  ? SPSC TryPush finds the ring full (head+1 == tail) -> task removed from ClientTaskManager, taskId invalidated, RESOURCE_BUSY "client task queue is full"  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:536-540
  ? prerequisiteEventHandle != 0 (only STORE/BATCH_STORE with event) -> runtime::SynchronizeEvent is called before processing; QUERY leaves it 0 so the gate is skipped  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:559-561
  ? worker device Setup failed -> every dequeued task goes to ClientTaskManager::CompleteWithError with deviceStatus; Process is never called  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:552-566
  ? opType is QUERY or DELETE in BuildTransportTasks -> keys are converted to std::string router keys and routed; retries/dedup not done, each key index appears once  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:273-275
  ? router type is CONTIGUOUS_BLOCK_AFFINITY -> RouteKeys anchors every blockCount consecutive keys to the node of the block's first key instead of hashing each key  @ kv_semantics/src/client/router/router.cc:248-264
  ? router type is BATCH_TOPK_AFFINITY -> RouteKeys selects TopK candidate nodes from a batch fingerprint, then maps each key within that candidate set  @ kv_semantics/src/client/router/router.cc:281-293
  ? a routed nodeId is absent from snapshot->transports -> build fails with NOT_FOUND "routed asu transport not found" (plus nodeId); the whole client task fails before dispatch  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:276-282
  ? RouteKeys returns no routes (e.g. empty key vector) -> DispatchTask immediately Finalizes the client task: OK status, empty exists vector, no transport IO  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:314-318
  ? transport->Submit fails at dispatch index k -> tasks before k keep running; index k gets the dispatch status; indices >k get CANCELED; Finalize when all are accounted for  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:342-347
  ? AsuTransportImpl::SubmitTask sees stopWorker_ or a non-joinable worker -> NOT_INITIALIZED "transport is not accepting tasks"; executeQueue push failure gives RESOURCE_BUSY  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:250-275
  ? QUERY key sub-batch split -> chunk size is queryIoNum_ (default 256), further lowered to server capabilities.queryKeys when the server advertises one  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/io_scheduler.cc:80-90
  ? transport attrs contain sc=false -> BuildExistRequest sets sc=false, SQE data[10] bit16 stays clear, subBatchContext.useSeekControl=false, completion uses existing_key_number prefix semantics  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:292
  ? transport attrs omit sc (default path) -> sc defaults to true, useSeekControl=true, completion consumes the per-key 1-bit result buffer and ignores existing_key_number  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:292
  ? Exist batch_number outside [1,256], unequal to keys.size(), rflag with zero response addr/mr key, or an all-zero key -> PackSqe returns INVALID_ARGUMENT; sub-batch build fails and the transport task completes as PARTIAL_FAILED  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:1005-1046
  ? Poll reads cid==0 or a cid different from the sub-batch cid -> completion is not consumed; the loop continues polling the same flag buffer next round  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:416-425
  ? CQE raw status is 0 (success) -> all query entries are set to ENTRY_KEY_EXIST without reading the result buffer or existing_key_number  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:146-149
  ? CQE raw status is CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER and useSeekControl==false (sc=false) -> existing_key_number is read from flag buffer data[0] low 16 bits; the first min(n, existing_key_number) entries become ENTRY_KEY_EXIST and all others ENTRY_KEY_NOT_EXIST (existing keys assumed to be a prefix); result_buffer is not read  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:151-157
  ? CQE raw status is CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER and useSeekControl==true (sc=true), result_buffer non-empty -> each entry status comes from the 1-bit buffer at flag buffer dword 4: bit 1 -> ENTRY_KEY_EXIST, bit 0 -> ENTRY_KEY_NOT_EXIST; existing_key_number is ignored  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:159-166
  ? CQE raw status is CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER and result_buffer is empty -> every entry is filled with the CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER error status (sub-batch still finishes OK because of the query normalization)  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:159-162
  ? sub-batch status maps to CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER for a QUERY -> effective completion status is forced to OK, so missing keys never fail the transport task  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:440-445
  ? mapped status is CQE_INTERNAL_ERROR or CQE_IO_TIMEOUT -> connManager_->ReportFailure (io_timeouts metric for timeout); all other statuses call ReportSuccess  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:446-454
  ? task deadline reached in Poll -> TIMEOUT filled into entryStatus, all PENDING sub-batches completed with TIMEOUT, task finalStatus=TIMEOUT  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:391-405
  ? QUERY transport result has no queryResult, or exists.size() != originalIndices.size() -> completionStatus becomes INTERNAL_ERROR (missing/size mismatch); entryStatus for those indices gets that error instead of the per-entry values  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:177-191
  ? any transport task finalStatus is non-OK at Finalize -> client finalStatus = PARTIAL_FAILED, otherwise OK  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:240-256
  ? Wait condition variable times out -> result.status=TIMEOUT but entryStatus/queryResult still carry the in-progress snapshot; the task is still removed from the manager  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:372-383
  ? Check/Wait is called for an already removed or never-issued taskId -> Check returns true; Wait returns TASK_NOT_FOUND  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:102-116

ERROR PATHS:
  ! Client not initialized / no transports: NOT_INITIALIZED at kv_client_impl.cc:498-501 and again at task_manager.cc:268-271, taskId set to kInvalidTaskId.
  ! Wrong opcode on keys overload: INVALID_ARGUMENT "keys submit only supports query/delete" at kv_client_impl.cc:503-507.
  ! Task context lost between Submit and Get: INTERNAL_ERROR "client task disappeared after submit" at kv_client_impl.cc:520-524.
  ! Shutdown raced submit: task removed and NotInitialized returned at kv_client_impl.cc:528-533.
  ! Client SPSC queue full: RESOURCE_BUSY "client task queue is full" at kv_client_impl.cc:536-540.
  ! Worker device Setup failure: every task completed through CompleteWithError at kv_client_impl.cc:552-566 (entryStatus filled with the device status).
  ! Routed node has no transport in snapshot: NOT_FOUND at task_manager.cc:276-282, whole task fails via CompleteWithError (task_manager.cc:141-145).
  ! Transport not accepting tasks / execute queue full / task vanished: NOT_INITIALIZED, RESOURCE_BUSY, INTERNAL_ERROR at kv_transport_impl.cc:253-272; dispatcher converts to CompleteUndispatchedTransportTasks (task_manager.cc:342-347).
  ! Undispatched transport tasks after a dispatch failure: first gets the dispatch error, later ones CANCELED at task_manager.cc:212-225.
  ! Empty key batch reaching the transport: INVALID_ARGUMENT "key batch is empty" at kv_submit_flow.cc:81-84; Execute aborts and BuildFinalStatus reports PARTIAL_FAILED (trans_task_manager.cc:32-41).
  ! SQE validation failure (batch out of [1,256], rflag/addr/mr-key mismatch, all-zero key): INVALID_ARGUMENT from kv_protocol.cc:1005-1046 during pack, sub-batch marked failed, transport result PARTIAL_FAILED.
  ! No connection channel: CONNECTION_ERROR at trans_task_executor.cc:290-300, connection_errors metric, sub-batch completed.
  ! Send returned wrong status count: INTERNAL_ERROR "transport send returned unexpected status count" at kv_submit_flow.cc:143-153; per-batch send failure marks the sub-batch failed and calls ReportFailure at 155-166.
  ! UnpackResponse failure: completeWithError(status) at trans_task_executor.cc:430-435 (unknown opcode -> INVALID_ARGUMENT).
  ! Transport deadline exceeded: TIMEOUT "transport task execution timeout" at trans_task_executor.cc:391-405.
  ! Transport task canceled during shutdown: CANCELED filled into all entry statuses at trans_task_executor.cc:258-277.
  ! Client transport completion with missing/mismatched query result: INTERNAL_ERROR "transport query result is missing" / "transport query result size mismatch" at task_manager.cc:177-185.
  ! One or more transport tasks failed: client finalStatus PARTIAL_FAILED "client task partially failed" at task_manager.cc:254-256.
  ! Client Wait timeout: TIMEOUT with client_task_id/op/wait_ms at task_manager.cc:375-382; task is removed at task_manager.cc:114, so later Wait returns TASK_NOT_FOUND.
  ! Wait on unknown/removed task: TASK_NOT_FOUND at task_manager.cc:111, surfaced before the view-refresh check at kv_client_impl.cc:313.

GOTCHAS:
  # useSeekControl is copied from the SQE sc field only in BuildSqeRequest's Exist case (sqe_request.cc:334-339), and sc defaults to TRUE when the attrs map has no "sc" (sqe_request.cc:292). So the default completion path is the per-key result buffer, not existing_key_number.
  # sc=true (useSeekControl=true): the code reads the flag/response buffer (subBatchContext.flagBuffer.local_addr) header dwords 0-3 (cid in dword 3, status in dword 3 bits 17+, existing_key_number in dword 0 low 16 bits) and then the 1-bit-per-key result buffer starting at dword 4 (kCqeDwordCount=4, kv_protocol.cc:30-56, 1053-1060). Entry bit 1 => ENTRY_KEY_EXIST, bit 0 => ENTRY_KEY_NOT_EXIST.
  # sc=false (useSeekControl=false) with CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER: result_buffer is ignored entirely; only response.existing_key_number (data[0] & 0xFFFF, kv_protocol.cc:1057) is used, and it is interpreted as a PREFIX count: the first min(entryCount, existing_key_number) keys are ENTRY_KEY_EXIST, all the rest ENTRY_KEY_NOT_EXIST (trans_task_utils.cc:151-157).
  # Query misses are not task failures: CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER is normalized to OK at trans_task_executor.cc:440-445, so TaskResult.status is OK while result.entryStatus holds ENTRY_KEY_NOT_EXIST for missing keys and queryResult.exists holds 0 for them.
  # prefixHitKeys returned by the transport (trans_task_utils.cc:169-180) is discarded by the client; CompleteTransportTask copies only exists, and Finalize recomputes prefixHitKeys by scanning the merged caller-ordered exists until the first 0 (task_manager.cc:232-238).
  # queryResult.exists is pre-sized at submit to keys.size() (kv_client_impl.cc:515) and later indexed via originalIndices (task_manager.cc:187-190), so the final vector is in the caller's key order even though routing groups keys per ASU and unordered_map iteration can reorder nodes.
  # ClientTask::keys is moved out and cleared in BuildTransportTasks (task_manager.cc:293-306); downstream stages only use TransportTask::keys, and the client task cannot be re-routed without re-submitting.
  # The view snapshot is captured at submit time (kv_client_impl.cc:512) and used for routing/dispatch, so a background view refresh after QueryAsync does not move an already-submitted task.
  # QUERY never sets prerequisiteEventHandle, so the WorkerLoop's SynchronizeEvent gate at kv_client_impl.cc:559-561 is always skipped for query; only StoreAsync/BatchStoreAsync with an event handle use it.
  # Transport task timeout is TransportConfig::timeoutMs (kv_transport_impl.cc:236, TaskDeadline at 42-46); 0 disables it (time_point::max). Client Wait timeout is separate: KvClientConfig::defaultWaitTimeoutMs when Wait's timeoutMs is 0 (kv_client_impl.cc:303), and UcmAsu rejects zero asu_query_timeout_ms (asu_store.cc:639-641).
  # Wait removes the task from ClientTaskManager even on timeout (task_manager.cc:113-114). The transport keeps running, but the onComplete callback holds only a weak_ptr to the ClientTask (task_manager.cc:324-329), so a late completion is silently dropped; subsequent Check returns true and Wait returns TASK_NOT_FOUND.
  # CompletionLoop polling is not interrupt-driven: PollResponseCid always returns OK and only cid==0/mismatch means 'not ready yet' (kv_transport_executor.cc:420-425, kv_protocol.cc:1239-1244); the loop sleeps 50us when no tasks and yields after completionPollSpinLimit idle rounds (kv_transport_impl.cc:286-313).
  # A transport task can complete from Execute rather than the completion loop: send-failed sub-batches are marked COMPLETED before InitializeRemainingSubBatchCount, so remainingSubBatchCount can be 0 immediately and BuildFinalStatus reports PARTIAL_FAILED (trans_task_executor.cc:352-375, kv_submit_flow.cc:143-166).
  # Transport-level result.status is only OK or PARTIAL_FAILED (BuildFinalStatus), not the original per-key/CQE error; the concrete CQE codes live in entryStatus and queryResult.exists only records existence.
  # queryIoNum is 256 by default (kv_types.h:199) but KvExistProtocol rejects batch_number > 256 (kv_protocol.cc:1007-1010); if the configured value exceeds the server's queryKeys capability the transport Init cap at kv_transport_impl.cc:130-138 is what keeps requests packable.
  # Entry-level unit ranges differ by op: QUERY statuses use 0x0300 base (ENTRY_KEY_NOT_EXIST / ENTRY_KEY_EXIST), DELETE 0x0200, batch load/store 0x0100 (trans_task_utils.cc:31-48).
  # SubmitKeySubBatchRequest dereferences BuildSqeRequest's result without a null check (sqe_request.cc:388-390); for QUERY the Exist case always returns a non-null request, so this is only reachable through an unsupported opcode.
  # Empty key vector sent to QueryAsync does not error: RouteKeys yields no routes, DispatchTask finalizes immediately with OK and an empty exists vector (task_manager.cc:314-318). The transport's "key batch is empty" error at kv_submit_flow.cc:81-84 is therefore defensive and only reachable via a transport task whose keys were emptied elsewhere.
  # Missing keys (sc=false) are inferred purely from a prefix count, so a server that reports non-contiguous existence via existing_key_number will produce wrong exists values; only sc=true preserves per-key positions.

==========================================================================================
TRACE 2: KvClient::DeleteAsync + Wait for AsuOpType::DELETE (keys path) — ClientTask -> TransportTask -> TransportSubBatch -> ASU Delete SQE -> CQE -> aggregation
==========================================================================================
ENTRYPOINTS: kv::KvClient::DeleteAsync (pure virtual) — kv_semantics/include/kv_client.h:50 | kv::KvClientImpl::DeleteAsync — kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:292-297 | kv::KvClientImpl::Wait — kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:301-319 | client producer/worker thread: KvClientImpl::WorkerLoop — kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:548-573 | transport submission (runs on client worker thread): AsuTransportImpl::Submit — kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:231-276 | transport worker thread: AsuTransportImpl::WorkerLoop — kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:278-284 | transport completion thread: AsuTransportImpl::CompletionLoop — kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:286-313 | UCM consumer (never issues DELETE): UC::AsuStore — ucm/store/asu/cc/asu_store.cc:400-440,758-773

--- PHASE: P1 — DeleteAsync submit (caller thread)  [thread: caller / producer thread] ---
  * DeleteAsync facade  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:292-297  :: Calls SubmitAsync(AsuOpType::DELETE, keys, taskId), then RecordSubmit(DELETE, keys.size(), status). Returns as soon as the task is enqueued (asynchronous); errors surface through Wait.
  * select DELETE metrics  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:50-85  :: GetSubmitMetrics maps DELETE to kv_client_delete_requests_total / kv_client_delete_entries_total / kv_client_delete_errors_total.
  * record submit metrics  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:87-98  :: Always adds requests+1 and entries+=keys.size(); adds errors+1 only if the submit status is not OK.
  * keys-path SubmitAsync validation  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:493-507  :: Rejects when snapshot/router/transports are missing (NOT_INITIALIZED) or opType is not QUERY/DELETE (INVALID_ARGUMENT).
  * build ClientTask  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:509-515  :: Sets opType=DELETE, captures the immutable ViewSnapshot, copies keys, initializes entryStatus to keys.size() OK entries, and assigns queryResult.exists with size 0 for DELETE.
  * register task and allocate client TaskId  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:517-524  :: taskManager_.Submit stores the shared ClientTask and assigns a TaskId; Get(taskId) re-fetches the same shared_ptr.
  * TaskManagerBase::Submit  @ kv_semantics/src/common/utils/task_manager_base.h:55-73  :: State is reset to PENDING; TaskId is a per-manager monotonic counter (never 0, collision-checked); task stored in an unordered_map under shared_mutex.
  * publish to SPSC queue  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:526-544  :: Under producerMu_: rejects if stopWorker_ is set (removes task, NOT_INITIALIZED), stamps enqueuedAt, TryPush into taskQueue_; on full removes the task and returns RESOURCE_BUSY; then notifies workerCv_ and records kv_client_task_enqueue_duration_seconds.
  * SPSC TryPush  @ kv_semantics/src/common/ds/spsc_ring_queue.h:73-82  :: Single-producer lock-free push; queue capacity is max(2,maxInflightTasks)+1 (kv_client_impl.cc:148-149,177).

--- PHASE: P2 — client worker: route keys and dispatch (1 ClientTask -> N TransportTasks)  [thread: client WorkerLoop thread] ---
  * pop task  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:548-573  :: ConsumerLoop spins then waits on workerCv_ (spsc_ring_queue.h:124-157); on shutdown the remaining queued tasks are drained before exit.
  * pre-process checks  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:553-566  :: Stamps processingStartedAt; DELETE has prerequisiteEventHandle==0, so no runtime::SynchronizeEvent; then either ClientTaskManager::Process or CompleteWithError for device setup failure.
  * ClientTaskManager::Process  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:134-154  :: State PENDING->INFLIGHT, BuildTransportTasks, then DispatchTask; records kv_client_task_process_duration_seconds on dispatch success.
  * convert keys for routing  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:52-58  :: ToRouterKeys builds one kv::Key (binary std::string) per 8-byte CacheKey via CacheKeyView.
  * route keys to ASUs  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:273-282  :: DELETE uses snapshot->router->RouteKeys(keys); routes is unordered_map<NodeId, vector<originalIndex>> (returns void when no keys). Any routed node without a transport in the snapshot yields NOT_FOUND.
  * Router::RouteKeys grouping  @ kv_semantics/src/client/router/router.cc:135-145  :: Each key is hashed (RingHash/Maglev/Contiguous/TopK implementation chosen by config); indices are appended per node in ascending caller order, node iteration order is unordered.
  * create per-node TransportTask  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:284-305  :: One TransportTask per routed node; keys are moved into task->keys, originalIndices records caller positions, and a weak_ptr to the AsuTransport is captured.
  * clear client task payload  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:305-309  :: Empties the client task's keys, then sets remainingTransportTasks and remainingTransportSendTasks to the transport-task count.
  * empty-routes shortcut  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:312-318  :: If transportTasks is empty (e.g. DeleteAsync with an empty key vector), Finalize is called immediately and no transport Submit occurs.
  * dispatch to each transport  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:320-349  :: Installs onComplete -> CompleteTransportTask and onSendComplete -> remainingTransportSendTasks accounting, copies opType, then calls transport->Submit(transportTask).
  * dispatch failure handling  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:341-347  :: If transport->Submit fails, DispatchTask returns that status after CompleteUndispatchedTransportTasks marks the failing task with the status and all later tasks CANCELED (task_manager.cc:207-228), then Finalize runs.

--- PHASE: P3 — transport enqueue (called from the client worker thread)  [thread: client WorkerLoop thread calling AsuTransportImpl] ---
  * AsuTransportImpl::Submit  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:231-238  :: Allocates task->entryStatus with entryCount = IsEntryBatchOp ? entries.size() : keys.size() (DELETE -> keys.size(), overwriting any prior statuses) and sets deadline = now + config_.timeoutMs (0 -> time_point::max, TaskDeadline at 42-46).
  * SubmitTask  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:250-276  :: Under transport producerMu_: rejects NOT_INITIALIZED when stopped/not joinable, assigns a transport-level TaskId via TransportTaskManager, re-fetches the task, TryPush into executeQueue_; full queue -> RESOURCE_BUSY and Remove.
  * transport WorkerLoop  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:278-284  :: Pops tasks; calls taskExecutor_->Execute(task); if Execute returns done (all sub-batches already terminal), immediately calls taskManager_.NotifyCompletion.

--- PHASE: P4 — split into sub-batches and build the Delete SQE  [thread: transport WorkerLoop thread] ---
  * Execute state CAS  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:307-313  :: TransportTask PENDING->INFLIGHT; if the task was already canceled/completed the CAS fails and Execute returns false.
  * PrepareTaskSubBatches (key op branch)  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:54-113  :: IsKeyBatchOp(DELETE): an empty key batch is rejected with INVALID_ARGUMENT (81-84); otherwise wraps keys in BatchView, calls ioScheduler_.SplitForAsu(keys, DELETE) and SubmitKeySubBatchRequest per sub-batch (86-98).
  * split by deleteIoNum  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/io_scheduler.cc:72-90  :: SplitBatchView cuts contiguous runs of GetSqeIoNum(DELETE)=deleteIoNum_; sub-batch count = 1 + (n-1)/ioNum. GetSqeIoNum returns 1 for LOAD/STORE, config values for batch ops.
  * effective deleteIoNum  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:130-142  :: Each server capability group applies min(config.deleteIoNum, capabilities.deleteKeys) when deleteKeys != 0; default deleteIoNum is 254 (kv_types.h:198).
  * SubmitKeySubBatchRequest  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:376-392  :: entryStatus sized to sub-batch keys; allocates a fresh cid (executor 279-284); prepares the flag buffer; builds the Delete request and packs it into a send buffer slot. No mrKey/entry buffer validation on the keys path.
  * flag buffer size (1 bit/key)  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:72-99  :: Delete (like Exist) uses 16-byte CQE header + ceil(batchNum/8) bytes; BatchStore/BatchRetrieve use 4 bits per entry.
  * build KvDeleteRequest  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:267-280  :: cid, kv_ns_id from transport attrs, response_buffer_addr/response_mr_key from the flag buffer, rflag=true, keys copied, batch_number=keys.size(); Delete has no dtype/dspec/lr and no sc attribute.
  * BuildSqeRequest dispatch  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:308-347  :: KvOpcode::Delete (from ToKvOpcode, trans_task_executor.h:41-53) requires source.keys and wraps BuildDeleteRequest.
  * pack Delete SQE  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:826-852  :: PackedSize = (16 + batch_number*4)*4 bytes. Header: dword0 = cid<<16 | kFixedBits<<14 | 0x8 | rflag<<13; dword1 = kv_ns_id; dwords3/4 = response addr lo/hi; dword5 = response mr key; dword8 = batch_number*16; dword9 = Batch<<24; dword10 = batch_number. Each 8-byte key is memcpy'd at dword 16+4*i (PackEntry 897-900).
  * validate Delete SQE  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:854-895  :: batch_number in [1,254], batch_number==keys.size(), rflag requires non-zero response addr and mr key, and no key may be all-zero.
  * allocate send slot and pack  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:139-167  :: GetPackedSize -> sendBufferManager_.Allocate -> ProtocolManager::PackRequest (kv_protocol.cc:1212-1226); any failure marks the sub-batch COMPLETED with that error via SetSubBatchBuildFailed (101-106).
  * assign connections  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:286-305  :: Each sub-batch selects a ConnectionChannel; no available channel marks that sub-batch CONNECTION_ERROR and aborts the pre-send phase.
  * build SendIoBatch list  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:115-128  :: One SendIoBatch{connectionHandle, sendBuffer device addr, flagBuffer device addr, sendSge.length} per sub-batch.

--- PHASE: P5 — send SQE to the ASU  [thread: transport WorkerLoop thread] ---
  * provider Send  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:130-167  :: Reads kernel_count/quiet_count attrs and calls transProvider_->Send(ioBatches, kernelCount, quietCount) once for all sub-batches of the transport task; AICPU groups by connection and launches HIXL batch send (aicpu_trans_provider.cc:1584-1653).
  * returned-status check  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:143-153  :: If the provider returns a status count != ioBatches.size(), every sub-batch is marked INTERNAL_ERROR and resources are released.
  * per-sub-batch send failure  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:155-166  :: A failed send marks the sub-batch COMPLETED with that status, calls connManager_->ReportFailure, and releases send/flag slots and the channel inflight slot.
  * send completion bookkeeping  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:339-350  :: Stamps sendCompletedAt, sets sendReturned, records send metrics, and invokes onSendComplete (client-side remainingTransportSendTasks accounting, task_manager.cc:330-339).
  * publish sub-batch contexts  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:352-376  :: Under task->mutex: if canceled during processing all resources are released and Execute returns false; otherwise subBatchContexts are moved onto the task, remainingSubBatchCount is initialized, and TryFinalizeFromSubBatches is called.
  * pre-send failure abort  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:227-246  :: AbortSubBatchesBeforeSend releases resources, fills entryStatus with the first failure / CANCELED for subsequent sub-batches, and marks all sub-batches COMPLETED.

--- PHASE: P6 — completion polling and Delete CQE decode  [thread: transport CompletionLoop thread] ---
  * completion loop  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:286-313  :: Each round snapshots taskManager_.GetAll(); for every task it calls taskExecutor_->Poll(ctx) and then taskManager_.NotifyCompletion(ctx) when Poll returns done. Idle rounds sleep 50us or yield after completionPollSpinLimit.
  * deadline check  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:391-405  :: If now >= task->deadline: every non-completed sub-batch gets TIMEOUT, connManager_->ReportFailure is called, and the task finalStatus is TIMEOUT.
  * poll response cid  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:416-425  :: Reads the flag buffer; PollResponseCid (kv_protocol.cc:1239-1244) loads dword3 with acquire and extracts cid = data[3] & 0xFFFF; responses with cid 0 or a mismatched cid are ignored this round.
  * unpack CQE  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:427-438  :: UnpackResponse(Delete) -> KvDeleteProtocol::UnpackCqe (kv_protocol.cc:902-908): UnpackCqeBase sets cid=data[3]&0xFFFF and status=(data[3]>>17)&0x7FFF (30-34); result bits come from UnpackResultBuffer1Bit at dword 4 (47-56).
  * map CQE status  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:104-137  :: raw 0x00 -> OK; any other raw value maps to CQE_* (0x10000|raw): e.g. 0x701 KEY_NOT_EXIST, 0x712 OUT_OF_CREATE_SIZE, 0x716 IO_TIMEOUT, 0x732 CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER, unknown -> IO_ERROR.
  * fill per-entry Delete statuses  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:139-167  :: CQE OK -> every entry OK (146-149). Other CQE without a result buffer -> every entry gets that CQE status (159-162). CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER with a result buffer -> per-entry ResultBufferEntryToStatus (164-166).
  * Delete raw-result mapping  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:55-99  :: For DELETE, raw 0x00 -> OK (57); raw 0x01 -> ENTRY_DELETE_FAILED with base 0x0200 (41-43, 77-85); anything else -> IO_ERROR. Entry raw values come from the 1-bit result buffer.
  * CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER is not success for Delete  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:440-455  :: queryResultBufferStatus is computed only for QUERY, so for DELETE a 0x732 CQE leaves sub-batch status non-OK. Only CQE_INTERNAL_ERROR / CQE_IO_TIMEOUT call ReportFailure (and IO timeout metric); all other codes including 0x732 call ReportSuccess.
  * complete sub-batch  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:248-256  :: CompleteSubBatch releases the send slot, flag slot and channel inflight token, marks the sub-batch COMPLETED with its status, and decrements remainingSubBatchCount.
  * finalize sub-batches  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:457  :: TryFinalizeFromSubBatches (trans_task_manager.cc:51-63) runs after each round; when remainingSubBatchCount reaches 0 it computes BuildFinalStatus and sets TransportTask COMPLETED.
  * transport final status  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:32-41  :: Empty sub-batch list -> PARTIAL_FAILED; otherwise any non-OK sub-batch status -> PARTIAL_FAILED 'transport task partially failed'; all OK -> OK.

--- PHASE: P7 — transport completion callback and client aggregation/Finalize  [thread: transport CompletionLoop thread invoking the client callback] ---
  * transport NotifyCompletion  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:65-84  :: Records kv_transport_task_e2e/completion metrics, builds TaskResult via BuildResult, invokes onComplete exactly once (completionNotified CAS, 23-30), then removes the transport task from its map.
  * transport BuildResult  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:86-104  :: result.status = task.finalStatus; sub-batch entryStatus vectors are flattened in sub-batch order into result.entryStatus; queryResult is reset and only built for QUERY (BuildQueryResultFromEntryStatus), so DELETE returns nullopt.
  * client CompleteTransportTask guard  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:168-200  :: Ignores duplicate completion (clientCompleted). The QUERY-only queryResult validation/merge block (177-192) is skipped for DELETE. Entry statuses are copied back to caller indices through transportTask->originalIndices (196-200).
  * decrement remaining transport tasks  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:202-204  :: fetch_sub; the thread that observes the count reach 1 (now 0) calls Finalize under task->waitMu.
  * client Finalize  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:230-264  :: QUERY-only prefix-hit computation (232-238) is skipped for DELETE. Counts transport tasks with non-OK finalStatus; aggregate is OK when zero, else PARTIAL_FAILED 'client task partially failed'. Records e2e metric, sets COMPLETED, and cv.notify_all.
  * whole-task error path  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:156-166  :: CompleteWithError fills every entryStatus with the error, sets finalStatus, records the metric, marks COMPLETED and notifies; used for build/routing failures before dispatch.
  * dispatch-failure path  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:207-228  :: Undispatched transport tasks are marked COMPLETED; the first gets the dispatch status, later ones CANCELED, each entryStatus is filled, and Finalize runs when the counter is zero.
  * transport shutdown cancel  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:193-203  :: After stopping the completion thread, each remaining task is Cancel()'d via the executor and NotifyCompletion is invoked, so client callbacks still fire with CANCELED.
  * executor Cancel semantics  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:258-277  :: Under task->mutex: pending sub-batches get CANCELED, resources released, task finalStatus=CANCELED, state COMPLETED.

--- PHASE: P8 — Wait returns the aggregate result and per-entry Delete statuses  [thread: caller thread] ---
  * KvClientImpl::Wait  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:301-319  :: timeoutMs==0 uses config_.defaultWaitTimeoutMs; delegates to ClientTaskManager::Wait; records kv_client_wait_requests_total and wait errors; TASK_NOT_FOUND is returned before any refresh check; otherwise status or any entry status may trigger a background view refresh.
  * ClientTaskManager::Wait  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:108-116  :: Get(taskId) or TASK_NOT_FOUND; WaitContext; then Remove(taskId) unconditionally, so a second Wait for the same id returns TASK_NOT_FOUND.
  * WaitContext blocking  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:366-384  :: cv.wait_for until Done or timeout; BuildResult always runs; on timeout result.status is replaced by TIMEOUT carrying client_task_id, op=delete and wait_ms, and the timeout is logged.
  * build TaskResult  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:352-364  :: result.status = finalStatus when Done, else IN_PROGRESS; entryStatus is copied; queryResult is assigned only for QUERY and reset (nullopt) for DELETE.
  * view refresh trigger  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:314-317  :: ShouldRefreshView(status) or ShouldRefreshView(result) starts RequestBackgroundRefresh; view_server.cc:129-146 returns true for CONNECTION_ERROR, IO_ERROR, TIMEOUT, NOT_FOUND and BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED in the aggregate status or any entry status.
  * result contents for DELETE  @ kv_semantics/include/kv_types.h:137-141  :: TaskResult carries status, entryStatus (per original key index) and optional queryResult; DELETE always leaves queryResult empty.

--- PHASE: P9 — UCM integration: AsuStore never issues DELETE  [thread: UCM caller] ---
  * StoreV1 contract has no delete  @ ucm/store/ucmstore_v1.h:40-177  :: StoreV1 exposes Setup, Readme, Lookup, LookupOnPrefix, LookupOnReverse, Prefetch, CheckHealth, Load, Dump, Check and Wait only; there is no delete/evict operation to map onto KvClient::DeleteAsync.
  * AsuStore Submit uses batch store/load only  @ ucm/store/asu/cc/asu_store.cc:400-440  :: Load()/Dump() call client_->BatchLoadAsync / client_->BatchStoreAsync; no other KvClient write path is used.
  * AsuStore lookup uses QueryAsync  @ ucm/store/asu/cc/asu_store.cc:758-773  :: Query blocks on QueryAsync + Wait(queryTimeoutMs) and consumes result.queryResult; the same Wait path would expose a DELETE result, but no caller exists.
  * AsuStore Wait wrapper  @ ucm/store/asu/cc/asu_store.cc:415-424  :: Wait forwards to client_->Wait and converts the aggregate status; it never inspects per-entry Delete statuses.
  * DeleteAsync call sites  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:292  :: Repo-wide references to DeleteAsync are only the API declaration/implementation, the kv_test runner (kv_semantics/kv_test/src/kv_client_runner.cc:83), and unit/e2e/asu_store fake tests. Nothing under ucm/store issues a delete.

BRANCHES:
  ? DeleteAsync called with an empty key vector -> RouteKeys returns no entries -> transportTasks empty -> DispatchTask calls Finalize immediately (OK) and no transport Submit/SQE is ever produced  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:273-275,314-318
  ? Snapshot missing, router missing, or no transports at submit time -> taskId = kInvalidTaskId and Status NOT_INITIALIZED returned synchronously  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:498-501
  ? Wrong opType passed to the keys-path SubmitAsync -> INVALID_ARGUMENT 'keys submit only supports query/delete', taskId invalid  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:503-507
  ? Client worker is stopping while the producer enqueues -> Task removed from client manager, taskId invalid, NOT_INITIALIZED returned  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:529-533
  ? Client SPSC taskQueue_ is full -> Task removed, taskId invalid, RESOURCE_BUSY returned  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:536-540
  ? Routed node has no transport in the captured snapshot -> BuildTransportTasks returns NOT_FOUND; CompleteWithError fills every client entryStatus with that status and completes the task  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:276-282,141-145,156-166
  ? Device setup or prerequisite event synchronize fails in the client worker -> ClientTaskManager::CompleteWithError marks the whole task failed with that status (DELETE has prerequisiteEventHandle 0, so only device setup can fail)  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:558-566
  ? AsuTransportImpl::Submit called while the transport is stopped or its worker is not joinable -> NOT_INITIALIZED returned; the client marks that transport task failed and later tasks CANCELED -> PARTIAL_FAILED  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:252-256
  ? Transport executeQueue_ is full -> Transport task removed, RESOURCE_BUSY returned to DispatchTask, which propagates the failure path  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:269-273
  ? DELETE reaches the transport with zero keys (defensive path, client normally short-circuits) -> PrepareTaskSubBatches returns INVALID_ARGUMENT 'key batch is empty' before any SQE is built  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:81-84
  ? Server advertises a deleteKeys capability lower than config.deleteIoNum -> effective deleteIoNum = min(config value (default 254), deleteKeys), increasing the number of Delete sub-batches  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:130-142
  ? Key count exceeds one deleteIoNum -> split into ceil(n/deleteIoNum) contiguous sub-batches, each with its own cid, flag buffer and send slot  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/io_scheduler.cc:31-52,72-90
  ? Flag buffer allocation fails for a Delete sub-batch -> SubBatchBuildFailed -> PrepareTaskSubBatches returns error -> AbortSubBatchesBeforeSend fills entryStatus and marks all sub-batches COMPLETED  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:83-99,125-137
  ? Delete SQE validation fails (batch_number 0 or >254, size mismatch, rflag/addr inconsistent, all-zero key) -> PackSubBatchRequest fails with INVALID_ARGUMENT and the sub-batch is marked COMPLETED with that error  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:854-895,139-167
  ? Send slot allocation or PackRequest fails -> sub-batch build error propagates, Execute takes the AbortSubBatchesBeforeSend path, task finalStatus becomes PARTIAL_FAILED  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:325-327,363,227-246
  ? No connection channel is available for a sub-batch -> AssignSubBatchConnections marks the sub-batch CONNECTION_ERROR and Execute aborts the pre-send phase  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:286-305
  ? TransProvider::Send returns a status count different from the io-batch count -> Every sub-batch is marked INTERNAL_ERROR and all send/flag slots and channel tokens are released  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:143-153
  ? TransProvider::Send reports a per-batch failure -> That sub-batch is COMPLETED with the provider status, connManager_->ReportFailure runs, resources are released  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:155-166
  ? Transport task deadline (config_.timeoutMs) elapses before all CQEs arrive -> All pending sub-batches get TIMEOUT, ReportFailure is called, finalStatus TASK TIMEOUT -> client PARTIAL_FAILED, entries TIMEOUT  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:391-405
  ? Delete CQE status is 0x00 -> All entries in the sub-batch get Status::OK (this is the only fully-successful Delete outcome)  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:146-149
  ? Delete CQE status is 0x732 (CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER) and a 1-bit result buffer is present -> Per key: raw 0 -> OK, raw 1 -> ENTRY_DELETE_FAILED (0x0201), any other -> IO_ERROR; sub-batch status stays 0x732  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:159-167,77-85
  ? Delete CQE is a non-zero error other than CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER (e.g. 0x701 KEY_NOT_EXIST, 0x716 IO_TIMEOUT, 0x712 OUT_OF_CREATE_SIZE) -> Every entry in the sub-batch receives that CQE status; only INTERNAL_ERROR/IO_TIMEOUT also call ReportFailure, all others ReportSuccess  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:437-455
  ? CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER with no result buffer data -> All entries get the CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER status itself (no per-entry split possible)  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:159-162

  ? All client TransportTasks report OK but some entryStatus are errors (e.g. fake/mock transport) -> Aggregate status stays OK; only per-entry statuses expose the failures (see kv_client_impl_test.cc:2062-2072)  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:240-256
  ? Duplicate transport completion callback -> Ignored: transport completionNotified CAS and client clientCompleted flag both guard against double completion  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:23-30
