  ? Wait/Check called for an unknown or already-waited taskId -> TASK_NOT_FOUND returned (Wait removes the task after the first successful/failed wait)  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:110-111
  ? Status or an entryStatus is CONNECTION_ERROR / IO_ERROR / TIMEOUT / NOT_FOUND / BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED -> Background view refresh is requested (in the worker after Process and in Wait after completion)  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:567,314-317
  ? Transport Shutdown while a Delete task is in flight -> Shutdown waits up to config_.timeoutMs, then executor Cancel marks pending sub-batches CANCELED and NotifyCompletion invokes the client callback  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:168-203

ERROR PATHS:
  ! Submit-stage NOT_INITIALIZED: no snapshot/router/transports at DeleteAsync time (kv_client_impl.cc:498-501) or client stopping (529-533).
  ! Submit-stage INVALID_ARGUMENT: keys-path SubmitAsync only accepts QUERY/DELETE (kv_client_impl.cc:503-507); transport rejects an empty key batch (kv_submit_flow.cc:81-84).
  ! Client queue pressure: taskQueue_ full -> RESOURCE_BUSY after removing the task (kv_client_impl.cc:536-540); transport executeQueue_ full -> RESOURCE_BUSY (kv_transport_impl.cc:269-273).
  ! Routing failure: a routed node has no transport in the snapshot -> NOT_FOUND, CompleteWithError fills all entryStatus (task_manager.cc:276-282,156-166).
  ! Partial dispatch: transport->Submit fails -> first failing task gets the status, all later tasks get CANCELED, aggregate PARTIAL_FAILED (task_manager.cc:341-347,207-228).
  ! Sub-batch build failure: flag buffer allocation, Delete SQE validation (batch range/size/rflag/all-zero key) or send buffer pack failure -> PARTIAL_FAILED with CANCELED for the remaining sub-batches (sqe_request.cc:83-167; kv_protocol.cc:854-895; trans_task_executor.cc:227-246).
  ! Connection failure: SelectConnection returns no channel -> every entry of that sub-batch gets CONNECTION_ERROR (trans_task_executor.cc:286-305).
  ! Provider send failure: status-count mismatch -> INTERNAL_ERROR for all sub-batches (kv_submit_flow.cc:143-153); per-batch failure -> that provider status plus ReportFailure (155-166).
  ! Timeout: transport deadline expires -> TIMEOUT for every pending sub-batch and the task (trans_task_executor.cc:391-405).
  ! Per-entry Delete failure: CQE 0x732 with result bits -> ENTRY_DELETE_FAILED (0x0201) for raw 1, IO_ERROR for unknown raw values; sub-batch stays non-OK so the task becomes PARTIAL_FAILED (trans_task_utils.cc:77-85,159-167; trans_task_manager.cc:32-41).
  ! CQE-level Delete error: any non-zero status other than 0x732 is copied to every entry (e.g. CQE_KEY_NOT_EXIST 0x10701, CQE_IO_TIMEOUT 0x10716, CQE_OUT_OF_CREATE_SIZE 0x10712) (trans_task_utils.cc:104-137,159-162).
  ! Overall task failure before dispatch: CompleteWithError fills all entries and sets finalStatus (task_manager.cc:156-166).
  ! Wait timeout: TIMEOUT with client_task_id/op=delete/wait_ms; the task is removed anyway (task_manager.cc:372-383,108-116).
  ! Unknown or already-consumed TaskId: TASK_NOT_FOUND (task_manager.cc:110-111; kv_client_impl.cc:313).
  ! Shutdown/cancel: transport Shutdown cancels in-flight tasks -> entryStatus and finalStatus CANCELED, client callback still fires (kv_transport_impl.cc:193-203; trans_task_executor.cc:258-277).

GOTCHAS:
  # DELETE uses only the keys overload (kv_client_impl.cc:493-546); it never resolves mrKey or touches registeredRegions_ (that code exists only in the KVBuffer/entries overload at 428-491). Delete therefore requires no caller buffer registration.
  # DELETE has no queryResult anywhere: the client sizes queryResult.exists to 0 at submit (kv_client_impl.cc:515), the transport BuildResult resets it (trans_task_manager.cc:100), and the client BuildResult resets it for every non-QUERY op (task_manager.cc:358-362). Wait returns result.queryResult == std::nullopt.
  # Delete-specific entry-status namespace: EntryStatusCode maps DELETE to base 0x0200 (trans_task_utils.cc:41-43), so raw 1 becomes ENTRY_DELETE_FAILED = 0x0201 (kv_types.h:71). This is distinct from batch ops (0x01xx) and query (0x03xx).
  # The Delete CQE result buffer is 1 bit per key (UnpackResultBuffer1Bit, kv_protocol.cc:47-56), and the flag buffer is 16 + ceil(n/8) bytes (sqe_request.cc:72-81). Batch store/load use 4 bits per entry.
  # CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER (0x732) is treated as success only for QUERY (trans_task_executor.cc:440-445). For DELETE it stays non-OK, so a batch with any failed key aggregates to PARTIAL_FAILED even though individual successful keys are OK.
  # PARTIAL_FAILED at the client is driven by transport task finalStatus, not by entryStatus. A mock/fake transport that returns OK with per-entry errors leaves the aggregate OK (kv_client_impl_test.cc:2062-2072). The real protocol turns any failed delete key into a 0x732 sub-batch status, so it does escalate.
  # Delete SQE layout differs from Query: no dtype/dspec/lr/sc fields; rflag is always true and response addr/mr_key always point at the flag buffer (sqe_request.cc:267-280; kv_protocol.cc:832-852). Query additionally encodes the sc attribute (sqe_request.cc:282-296).
  # Splitting differs by op: DELETE uses deleteIoNum (default 254, kv_types.h:198) capped by server deleteKeys (kv_transport_impl.cc:130-142); QUERY uses queryIoNum (default 256). Protocol hard limits are 254 for Delete and 256 for Exist (kv_protocol.cc:855-859 and kMax* constants at kv_protocol.h:55-57).
  # Routing is by 8-byte CacheKey hashed as a binary std::string (task_manager.cc:52-58; router.cc:135-145). Routes is an unordered_map, so the order in which per-node TransportTasks are submitted is unspecified; per-node key order preserves caller order via originalIndices (task_manager.cc:290-295).
  # AsuTransportImpl::Submit overwrites task->entryStatus with keys.size() OK entries (kv_transport_impl.cc:234-235), so nothing carried from the client task survives into the transport task.
  # There are three threads per Delete: one client worker (submit/route/dispatch), one transport worker (build sub-batches + send), one transport completion poller (CQE decode + finalize). The client callback runs on the transport completion thread (or the transport worker if everything failed synchronously).
  # Transport deadline is config_.timeoutMs, and 0 means infinite (kv_transport_impl.cc:42-46,236). The client Wait timeout (config_.defaultWaitTimeoutMs) is independent; a Wait timeout does not cancel the transport task, and the client task is removed from the map regardless.
  # Each manager has its own TaskId counter: the client TaskId returned by DeleteAsync is unrelated to the transport TaskId printed in transport logs (task_manager_base.h:66-71).
  # Transport task lifetime is protected by the snapshot: retired transports are kept in retiredTransports_ until Shutdown so old in-flight Delete tasks can still complete (kv_client_impl.cc:852-857,198-230).
  # UCM never uses this path: StoreV1 has no delete operation (ucm/store/ucmstore_v1.h:40-177) and AsuStore only calls BatchLoadAsync/BatchStoreAsync/QueryAsync (ucm/store/asu/cc/asu_store.cc:427-440,758-773). DeleteAsync is exercised only by kv_test (kv_semantics/kv_test/src/kv_client_runner.cc:83), the smoke/metrics tests, and the asu_store test fake.

==========================================================================================
TRACE 3: KvClient::StoreAsync / LoadAsync end-to-end for AsuOpType::STORE / AsuOpType::LOAD, single-entry path: KVBuffer[1] -> ClientTask -> TransportTask (1 node) -> 1 TransportSubBatch (I
==========================================================================================
ENTRYPOINTS: kv::KvClient::LoadAsync(const std::vector<KVBuffer>&, TaskId&) -- kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:252-257 (no event handle parameter) | kv::KvClient::StoreAsync(const std::vector<KVBuffer>&, TaskId&, std::uintptr_t eventHandle) -- kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:259-265 | kv::KvClient::StoreAsync(const std::vector<KVBuffer>&, TaskId&) -- kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:267-270 (forwards eventHandle=0) | Public interface declarations -- kv_semantics/include/kv_client.h:42-45 | Shared entry path KvClientImpl::SubmitAsync(AsuOpType, entries, taskId, eventHandle) -- kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:428-491

--- PHASE: Phase 1: Entry call, metrics, ClientTask construction, mrKey resolution, enqueue  [thread: caller thread] ---
  * LoadAsync forwards to the shared entry submitter  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:252-257  :: SubmitAsync(AsuOpType::LOAD, entries, taskId) uses the default eventHandle=0 (declared at kv_client_impl.h:117), so LOAD can never carry a prerequisite event; then RecordSubmit(LOAD, entries.size(), status).
  * StoreAsync forwards to the shared entry submitter with an event handle  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:259-265  :: 3-arg overload calls SubmitAsync(AsuOpType::STORE, entries, taskId, eventHandle), then RecordSubmit(STORE, ...). The 2-arg overload (267-270) calls the 3-arg one with 0. This is the only API asymmetry between LOAD and STORE at the facade.
  * Per-op submit metrics selected and recorded  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:50-98  :: GetSubmitMetrics maps LOAD -> kv_client_load_{requests,entries,errors}_total, STORE -> kv_client_store_{requests,entries,errors}_total. RecordSubmit increments requests=1, entries=entryCount and errors=1 only when status is not ok.
  * Hard precondition: client initialized and has a routable snapshot  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:431-436  :: GetSnapshot() under mutex_ returns null unless initialized_; snapshot must also have a router and non-empty transports, else taskId=kInvalidTaskId and NOT_INITIALIZED 'client has no ASU transports'.
  * Op-type gate for the entries overload  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:438-443  :: Only LOAD, STORE, BATCH_LOAD, BATCH_STORE are accepted; anything else returns INVALID_ARGUMENT 'entries submit only supports load/store'.
  * ClientTask (level 1 of 3) allocated  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:445-450  :: ctx->submittedAt=taskStart, opType, prerequisiteEventHandle=eventHandle, viewSnapshot=snapshot (pins the immutable routing/transport set), entries copied. Struct at kv_semantics/src/common/utils/task_context.h:125-150.
  * mrKey resolution: caller value discarded, token looked up by buffer.handle  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:451-459  :: Under memoryMu_: entry.mrKey.reset() first (comment: 'mrKey is client-owned metadata. Ignore any value supplied by the caller.'), then registeredRegions_.find(entry.buffer.handle); on hit entry.mrKey = iter->second.tokenId. On miss the entry keeps an empty optional and will later be rejected by the transport with BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED.
  * Client-side entry status vector seeded  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:460  :: ctx->entryStatus.assign(entries.size(), Status::OK()); for a single entry this is one OK slot that will be overwritten by the completion path.
  * Aggregate TaskId allocated  @ kv_semantics/src/common/utils/task_manager_base.h:55-73  :: TaskManagerBase::Submit sets state=PENDING, allocates a unique non-zero TaskId via nextTaskId_++, stores ctx in the map. This TaskId is what the caller later passes to Check/Wait.
  * Task pushed onto the client SPSC ring and worker signalled  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:465-489  :: Get(taskId) re-fetches the shared_ptr; under producerMu_ it checks stopWorker_ (Remove + NOT_INITIALIZED) and TryPush into taskQueue_ (Remove + RESOURCE_BUSY on full), stamps rawCtx->enqueuedAt, notifies workerCv_, and records kv_client_task_enqueue_duration_seconds.

--- PHASE: Phase 2: Client WorkerLoop pick-up and prerequisite-event gate  [thread: client WorkerLoop (single thread, KvClientImpl::WorkerLoop)] ---
  * ConsumerLoop pops the task and runs processTask  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:548-573  :: WorkerLoop sets up runtime::Device from config_.transportConfigs.front().deviceId once (550-552). Per task: stamp processingStartedAt, record kv_client_task_queue_duration_seconds (555-557).
  * Prerequisite event synchronization (STORE only in practice)  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:558-566  :: If device setup ok and ctx->prerequisiteEventHandle != 0, call runtime::SynchronizeEvent(handle) (declared kv_semantics/src/common/runtime/event.h:32, aclrtSynchronizeEvent in ascend_device.cc:36-39). Because LoadAsync never supplies an event handle, this branch is exclusive to Store/BatchStore. On failure, CompleteWithError instead of Process.
  * ClientTaskManager::Process: PENDING -> INFLIGHT, build + dispatch  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:134-154  :: state=INFLIGHT, BuildTransportTasks (routing), then DispatchTask; on dispatch success records kv_client_task_process_duration_seconds.

--- PHASE: Phase 3: Routing into TransportTask(s) (level 2 of 3)  [thread: client WorkerLoop] ---
  * Keys extracted from the entry batch  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:60-66  :: ExtractEntryKeys converts each KVBuffer.key into kv::Key(std::string from the 8-byte CacheKeyView) for the entries path; Query/Delete use the keys path instead.
  * Router::RouteKeys maps each entry index to an ASU node  @ kv_semantics/src/client/router/router.cc:135-145  :: Returns unordered_map<NodeId, vector<EntryIndex>>; keys routing to kInvalidNodeId are silently dropped. Called at task_manager.cc:273-275 for LOAD/STORE.
  * All routed nodes must still have a transport in the pinned snapshot  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:276-282  :: Missing transport -> NOT_FOUND 'routed asu transport not found, nodeId=...' before any task is created.
  * One TransportTask per ASU; entries moved into it  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:284-304  :: For a single entry there is one TransportTask with one entry and originalIndices=[0]; the entry is moved out of task->entries (line 299) and the client vector is swapped empty at 305. remainingTransportTasks and remainingTransportSendTasks are both initialized to transportTasks.size() at 307-308.

--- PHASE: Phase 4: Dispatch to AsuTransport and enqueue onto the transport ring  [thread: client WorkerLoop] ---
  * Callbacks installed, opType copied, transport->Submit called  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:312-350  :: onComplete (325-329) maps back through CompleteTransportTask(taskIndex); onSendComplete (330-339) decrements remainingTransportSendTasks and, when it reaches 0, records kv_client_task_send_duration_seconds; transportTask->opType = task->opType (LOAD or STORE).
  * AsuTransportImpl::Submit seeds per-entry status and deadline  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:231-238  :: entryCount = entries.size() for IsEntryBatchOp; task->entryStatus.assign(entryCount, OK); task->deadline = now + config_.timeoutMs (TaskDeadline returns time_point::max() only when timeoutMs==0, lines 42-46).
  * Transport task registered and pushed to executeQueue_  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:250-276  :: Under producerMu_: reject if stopWorker_/worker not joinable (NOT_INITIALIZED), allocate a TransportTaskId via TransportTaskManager::Submit, re-fetch it, TryPush into executeQueue_, notify workerCv_.

--- PHASE: Phase 5: Transport WorkerLoop, sub-batch split (level 3 of 3)  [thread: transport WorkerLoop] ---
  * ConsumerLoop runs Execute and completes inline if done  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:278-284  :: taskExecutor_->Execute(task) returning true means the task already completed synchronously (e.g. pre-send abort/cancel), so NotifyCompletion is invoked from the worker thread; otherwise CompletionLoop owns it.
  * Execute: PENDING -> INFLIGHT CAS and metric handle capture  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:307-321  :: compare_exchange PENDING->INFLIGHT; task->asuCompletionMetric = &asuCompletionMetric_ (node-tagged metric, constructed at 45-50); PrepareTaskSubBatches then AssignSubBatchConnections.
  * Entry batch routed to the entry sub-batch path  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:54-79  :: IsEntryBatchOp(opType) true for LOAD/STORE; empty batch -> INVALID_ARGUMENT 'entry batch is empty'; BatchView over ctx.entries; ioScheduler_.SplitForAsu(entries, opType); one BuildEntrySubBatchRequest per sub-batch.
  * ioNum for LOAD/STORE is hard-coded to 1, so each entry is its own sub-batch  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/io_scheduler.cc:80-90  :: GetSqeIoNum returns 1 for AsuOpType::LOAD and AsuOpType::STORE (batch ops use batchLoadIoNum_/batchStoreIoNum_, defaults 110 at io_scheduler.h:54-55). SplitBatchView (36-52) therefore emits one ScheduledIoBatch of size 1 per entry; with one entry there is exactly one sub-batch.

--- PHASE: Phase 6: Build one Store/Retrieve SQE (cid, flag buffer, 64B send packet, validation)  [thread: transport WorkerLoop] ---
  * BuildEntrySubBatchRequest: status vector, opcode mapping, mrKey validation  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:351-368  :: subBatchContext.entryStatus.assign(1, OK); opcode = ToKvOpcode(opType) (LOAD->Retrieve 0x2, STORE->Store 0x1, trans_task_executor.h:41-53 / kv_protocol.h:37-45); ValidateSqeMrKeys rejects any entry whose mrKey is empty with BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED (45-55); cid = AllocateRequestCid() (trans_task_executor.cc:279-284, 16-bit, skips 0); PrepareSubBatchRequest allocates the flag buffer.
  * Flag buffer allocation sized by opcode  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:72-137  :: GetFlagBufferSize default branch (Store/Retrieve/KeepAlive) = 16-byte header + 1 = 17 bytes; AllocateSubBatchFlagBuffer gets a slot from flagBufferManager_ (default slot capacity 71, kv_types.h:194); subBatchContext.opType and cid are stamped at 129-130.
  * Single-entry request object built (STORE vs LOAD divergence)  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:308-347  :: KvOpcode::Store requires entries.size==1 then BuildStoreRequest (196-213: cid, kv_ns_id, dtype, dspec from transport attrs, buffer_addr=region.addr, buffer_length=region.size, mr_key=*mrKey, offset=entry.offset, lr, length=buffer_length, key). KvOpcode::Retrieve requires entries.size==1 then BuildRetrieveRequest (215-230: same set minus dtype/dspec, which do not exist on KvRetrieveRequest). kv_ns_id/dtype/dspec/lr come from GetTransportConfigAttr over config_.attrs (57-70).
  * Packed size = 16 dwords (64 bytes) for both opcodes  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:1212-1226  :: ProtocolManager::GetPackedSize/PackRequest dispatch through the per-opcode KvProtocol objects registered in the ctor (1201-1210). PackedSize is kSqeDwordCount*4 = 64 (kv_protocol.h:196-199 Store, 211-214 Retrieve).
  * Send-buffer slot allocated and SQE packed  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:139-167  :: sendBufferManager_.Allocate(packedSize, subBatchContext.sendSge) (default slot capacity 4160, kv_types.h:191), then protocolManager_.PackRequest(sendSge.local_addr, opcode, request); any failure calls SetSubBatchBuildFailed and returns.
  * 24-bit length and 512B alignment validation, STORE  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:183-230  :: ValidateRequest rejects: buffer_addr==0; buffer_length==0 / not %512 / >0xFFFFFF; offset not %512; length==0 ('1-based') / >0xFFFFFF; all-zero key; dtype>0x7; dspec>0x1F. kAlignmentBytes=512 (kv_types.h:43).
  * 24-bit length and 512B alignment validation, LOAD  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:322-360  :: Identical checks except dtype/dspec (KvRetrieveRequest has no such fields).
  * SQE bit layout (STORE)  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:162-181  :: target[0]=(cid<<16)|(0x3<<14)|0x1; target[1]=kv_ns_id; target[2]=((dtype&0x7)<<13)|((dspec&0x1F)<<8); target[6..7]=buffer_addr; target[8]=((mr_key&0xFF)<<24)|(buffer_length&0xFFFFFF); target[9]=(DptrType::Standard 0x40)<<24 | (mr_key>>8); target[10]=offset; target[11]=(lr<<31)|(length&0xFFFFFF); target[12..] = 8-byte key. Note rflag bit 13 is NOT set for single Store.
  * SQE bit layout (LOAD)  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:302-320  :: target[0] uses opcode 0x2; target[2] is left zero (dtype/dspec absent); the rest is identical, i.e. offset=entry.offset and length=buffer_length(=region.size) in target[10]/target[11].

--- PHASE: Phase 7: Connection selection, provider Send, onSendComplete metric  [thread: transport WorkerLoop] ---
  * One connection channel per sub-batch  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:286-305  :: connManager_->SelectConnection(); null -> CONNECTION_ERROR, entryStatus filled, sub-batch COMPLETED with that status and Execute aborts pre-send.

  * Provider Send and per-batch failure handling  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:130-167  :: Reads kernel_count/quiet_count from config_.attrs, calls transProvider_->Send(ioBatches,...), records kv_transport_task_send_call_duration_seconds; status-count mismatch -> INTERNAL_ERROR on all sub-batches; per-batch failure -> SetSubBatchSendFailed + connManager_->ReportFailure + ReleaseSubBatchResources.
  * Pre-send/send metrics and onSendComplete callback  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:328-350  :: ioBatches non-empty: records kv_transport_task_pre_send_duration_seconds, _queue_duration_seconds, _process_duration_seconds, calls SendSubBatchBuffers, stamps sendCompletedAt and sendReturned=true, records kv_transport_task_send_duration_seconds plus the node-labeled asuSendMetric_ ('kv_transport_node_task_send_duration_seconds'), then invokes task->onSendComplete(), which is the client-level kv_client_task_send_duration_seconds metric installed at task_manager.cc:330-339.
  * Sub-batch contexts published for the polling thread  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:352-376  :: Under task->mutex: if already Done (canceled) release everything and return false; on pre-send failure call AbortSubBatchesBeforeSend (227-246: first failed status then CANCELED for the rest, all state COMPLETED); move subBatchContexts into task->subBatchContexts; InitializeRemainingSubBatchCount (trans_task_manager.cc:43-49) and TryFinalizeFromSubBatches (51-63) handle the already-complete case.

--- PHASE: Phase 8: CompletionLoop polling, CQE unwrap, entryStatus fill  [thread: transport CompletionLoop (separate thread)] ---
  * Round-robin poll over every registered transport task  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:286-313  :: Sleeps 50us when no tasks; otherwise taskExecutor_->Poll(ctx) per task; on true -> taskManager_.NotifyCompletion(ctx). Spin limiter uses config_.completionPollSpinLimit (default 16, kv_types.h:182).
  * Deadline check before touching CQEs  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:391-405  :: now >= task->deadline -> kv_transport_task_completion_timeouts_total, all entryStatus = TIMEOUT, connManager_->ReportFailure per channel, every pending sub-batch completed, task COMPLETED.
  * CQE cid peek from the flag buffer (acquire load)  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:1239-1244  :: PollResponseCid reads __atomic_load_n(data+3) & 0xFFFF from the flag buffer's host-visible local_addr (trans_task_executor.cc:417-418). 0 or != subBatchContext.cid means 'not our completion yet', so the loop continues (trans_task_executor.cc:425).
  * CQE unwrap  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:1228-1237, 30-34  :: UnpackResponse dispatches to KvStoreProtocol/KvRetrieveProtocol::UnpackCqe (232-236 / 362-367), which call UnpackCqeBase: out.cid = data[3] & 0xFFFF; out.status = (data[3] >> 17) & 0x7FFF. No result_buffer is produced for single Store/Retrieve.
  * Raw CQE status -> framework StatusCode  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:104-137  :: 0x00 -> OK; otherwise StatusCode = 0x10000 | raw (CQE_KEY_NOT_EXIST 0x701, CQE_IO_TIMEOUT 0x716, CQE_RESOURCE_BUSY 0x731, CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER 0x732, ...).
  * entryStatus fill from CQE  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:139-167  :: Wrapper trans_task_manager.cc:9-14 passes opType/useSeekControl from the sub-batch. status.ok() -> all entries OK (147). Otherwise, for non-QUERY ops, since result_buffer is empty for Store/Retrieve, the branch at 159-162 fills every entry with the CQE status.
  * Channel health feedback and sub-batch completion  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:437-457  :: CQE_INTERNAL_ERROR / CQE_IO_TIMEOUT -> connManager_->ReportFailure (plus kv_transport_task_io_timeouts_total for timeout), else ReportSuccess; CompleteSubBatch (248-256) releases the send slot, flag slot and channel inflight, marks COMPLETED, decrements remainingSubBatchCount; TryFinalizeFromSubBatches closes the transport task when the count hits 0.

--- PHASE: Phase 9: Transport completion -> ClientTaskManager callback (status mapping back to entry indices)  [thread: transport CompletionLoop (or transport WorkerLoop when Execute returned done)] ---
  * TransportTaskManager::NotifyCompletion  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:65-84  :: Records kv_transport_task_e2e_duration_seconds and kv_transport_task_completion_duration_seconds (+ node-labeled asuCompletionMetric_) only when sendReturned; BuildResult; task->NotifyCompletion fires onComplete once (completionNotified guard); task removed from the transport map.
  * Transport result assembled from sub-batch entry statuses  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:86-104  :: result.status = Task.finalStatus; entryStatus concatenated in sub-batch order (single entry -> one status); queryResult reset for non-QUERY.
  * ClientTaskManager::CompleteTransportTask maps transport indices back  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:168-205  :: Idempotent via transportTask->clientCompleted; task->entryStatus[originalIndices[i]] = result.entryStatus[i] (or completionStatus if the transport result is short, 196-200); decrement remainingTransportTasks; the last one calls Finalize.
  * ClientTaskManager::Finalize  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:230-264  :: Counts failed transport tasks and logs each (client_task_id, nodeId, task_id, item_count); finalStatus = OK if none failed else PARTIAL_FAILED 'client task partially failed'; RecordClientTaskCompletion (79-88, kv_client_task_e2e_duration_seconds, once via completionMetricRecorded); state=COMPLETED; cv.notify_all().

--- PHASE: Phase 10: Caller observe / Wait  [thread: caller thread] ---
  * KvClientImpl::Wait  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:301-319  :: timeoutMs==0 -> config_.defaultWaitTimeoutMs; taskManager_.Wait; records kv_client_wait_requests_total / kv_client_wait_errors_total; TASK_NOT_FOUND returned early; otherwise view refresh may be requested based on status/result.
  * ClientTaskManager::Wait / WaitContext / BuildResult  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:108-116, 352-384  :: cv.wait_for until Done; BuildResult copies finalStatus and entryStatus (queryResult only for QUERY); timeout -> StatusCode::TIMEOUT with the diagnostic message; the task is removed from the map after the wait (114).

BRANCHES:

ERROR PATHS:
  ! Not initialized / no snapshot: kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:432-436 (NOT_INITIALIZED) and kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:267-271 (same guard re-checked in the worker).

  ! Shutdown race: kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:474-478 removes the task and returns NOT_INITIALIZED; queue full at 481-485 returns RESOURCE_BUSY.
  ! Prerequisite event sync failure (STORE only): kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:559-565 -> ClientTaskManager::CompleteWithError (kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:156-166) sets every entryStatus and finalStatus then notifies.
  ! Unregistered buffer: kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:45-55 + 359-363 -> BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED, sub-batch completed before send; surfaces as PARTIAL_FAILED at kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:254-256.
  ! Flag buffer exhaustion: kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:83-99 (BufferManager::Allocate returns RESOURCE_BUSY from kv_semantics/src/trans/buffer/buffer_manager.cc:176-179).
  ! Send buffer exhaustion / pack failure: kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:139-167 (setSubBatchBuildFailed).
  ! SQE validation: 512B alignment / 24-bit length / zero key / dtype>7 / dspec>0x1F -> kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:183-230 (Store) and 322-360 (Retrieve).
  ! No connection channel: kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:289-300 -> CONNECTION_ERROR, kv_transport_task_connection_errors_total.
  ! Pre-send abort: kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:325-327 + 363 + 227-246; entries become CANCELED even if their own sub-batch was fine.
  ! Provider Send failure: kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:143-153 (count mismatch -> INTERNAL_ERROR for all) and 155-166 (per-batch status + ReportFailure).
  ! Cancellation: kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:258-277 (Cancel) and 353-361 (Execute observes Done and releases resources).
  ! Completion timeout: kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:391-405 -> TIMEOUT, kv_transport_task_completion_timeouts_total. Note transport timeoutMs defaults to 100ms (kv_semantics/include/kv_types.h:180).
  ! CQE unpack failure: kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:430-435 -> sub-batch completed with that error.

  ! Dispatch failure after the first transport task: kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:342-347 + 207-228 -> first task gets the dispatch error, remaining tasks CANCELED.

GOTCHAS:
  # Asymmetry is at the facade only: only StoreAsync has an eventHandle overload (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:259-265, 267-270); LoadAsync (252-257) cannot pass one, so the SynchronizeEvent gate at kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:559-561 is STORE-only even though ClientTask carries the field for all ops.
  # mrKey is not a caller input: it is unconditionally reset and recomputed from registeredRegions_[entry.buffer.handle].tokenId under memoryMu_ (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:451-459). A caller-supplied mrKey is silently discarded; a handle that is registered but missing from the map produces BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED only later in the transport.
  # There is no per-entry length field: KVBuffer only has key/buffer/offset (kv_semantics/include/kv_types.h:122-128), and both BuildStoreRequest and BuildRetrieveRequest set buffer_length = length = entry.buffer.region.size (kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:206-210, 223-227). The transfer length is therefore the whole registered region, not a slice.
  # ioNum=1 is op-type based, not config based: IoScheduler::GetSqeIoNum returns 1 for LOAD/STORE before consulting batchLoadIoNum_/batchStoreIoNum_ (kv_semantics/src/trans/task/io_scheduler.cc:80-90), so a multi-entry LoadAsync to one node still becomes one TransportTask with N sub-batches and N cids, never one batch SQE.
  # Store vs Load SQE divergence: opcode 0x1 vs 0x2 (kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.h:37-45, mapping in trans_task_executor.h:41-53); STORE additionally packs dtype (3 bits) and dspec (5 bits) into target[2] and validates their ranges (kv_protocol.cc:170, 219-228), while Retrieve leaves target[2] = 0 and VerifyPackedBuffer requires it to be zero (kv_protocol.cc:385-388). offset/length are encoded identically (target[10], target[11]).
  # Neither single Store nor single Retrieve sets rflag or a response_buffer_addr/response_mr_key in the SQE (kv_protocol.cc:168, 308 vs BatchStore at 448-453), yet the transport still allocates a 17-byte flag buffer per sub-batch (sqe_request.cc:72-81) and polls the CQE from flagBuffer.local_addr (trans_task_executor.cc:417-418). In-repo proof that providers write the CQE there exists only in the fake provider (kv_semantics/src/trans_provider/fake/fake_trans_provider.cc:481-509, PackCqeHeader at 117-123); the production AIV Send is out-of-repo (libasu_transport.so).
  # Null-dereference latent bug: BuildSqeRequest returns nullptr for Store/Retrieve when entries.size != 1 (sqe_request.cc:315-321) and BuildEntrySubBatchRequest dereferences the unique_ptr unconditionally (sqe_request.cc:370-373). Current safety comes solely from ioNum=1.
  # The flag-buffer slot size depends on opcode: GetFlagBufferSize's default branch gives 16+1 bytes for Store/Retrieve/KeepAlive, while Batch ops use 16+(n+1)/2 and key ops 16+(n+7)/8 (sqe_request.cc:72-81); slot capacity defaults to 71 (kv_types.h:194).
  # CQE is unwrapped from dword 3 only: cid = data[3] & 0xFFFF, status = (data[3] >> 17) & 0x7FFF (kv_protocol.cc:30-34). The cid gate is an acquire atomic load of the same dword (kv_protocol.cc:1239-1244) and a mismatch just means 'not ready', never an error.
  # For single Store/Retrieve, UnpackCqe does not fill result_buffer (kv_protocol.cc:232-236, 362-367), so CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER cannot be decoded per entry: FillEntryStatusFromCqeResult falls through to copying the sub-batch status into every entry (kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:159-162). Per-entry result decoding only exists for BatchStore/BatchLoad/Delete/Query.
  # Two distinct 'finalize' steps: TransportTask::TryFinalizeFromSubBatches (kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:51-63) closes the level-2 task; ClientTaskManager::Finalize (kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:230-264) closes the level-1 aggregate and is what computes PARTIAL_FAILED.
  # onSendComplete fires only when ioBatches is non-empty (trans_task_executor.cc:328-350). A task that aborted before send never decrements remainingTransportSendTasks, so kv_client_task_send_duration_seconds is not recorded for it (kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:330-339).
  # After BuildTransportTasks the ClientTask's entries/keys vectors are swapped empty (kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:305-306); anything reading task->entries after dispatch (e.g. logging at trans_task_executor.cc:369-372 sees TransportTask entries) must use the transport task, not the client task.
  # Two threads can deliver completion: Execute returning true calls NotifyCompletion from the transport WorkerLoop (kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:282), otherwise the CompletionLoop does (300-302). TransportTask::NotifyCompletion's completionNotified exchange guarantees onComplete runs once (trans_task_manager.cc:23-30).
  # Deadline semantics: TransportConfig.timeoutMs defaults to 100 ms (kv_semantics/include/kv_types.h:180) and TaskDeadline maps 0 to 'no deadline' (kv_transport_impl.cc:42-46), so a legitimately slow CQE yields StatusCode::TIMEOUT entries rather than a hang.

TRACE 4: KvClient::BatchStoreAsync (with eventHandle and without) and KvClient::BatchLoadAsync traced end-to-end: KvClientImpl -> ClientTaskManager (one WorkerLoop thread) -> per-ASU AsuTra

  * BatchLoadAsync forwards to SubmitAsync with BATCH_LOAD and has no eventHandle parameter at all  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:272-277  :: AsuOpType::BATCH_LOAD; counterpart of the kv_client.h:46 interface (no event overload exists for batch load).
  * RecordSubmit chooses batch-specific metric names and counts requests/entries/errors  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:68-98  :: BATCH_LOAD -> kv_client_batch_load_requests_total/entries_total/errors_total; BATCH_STORE -> kv_client_batch_store_*; UpdateStats applies 2 counters on OK, 3 on error.
  * SubmitAsync rejects a missing/empty view snapshot or zero transports  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:432-436  :: taskId=kInvalidTaskId; StatusCode::NOT_INITIALIZED "client has no ASU transports".
  * SubmitAsync restricts this overload to LOAD/STORE/BATCH_LOAD/BATCH_STORE  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:438-443  :: Any other op -> StatusCode::INVALID_ARGUMENT "entries submit only supports load/store".
  * Creates a ClientTask, stores opType, prerequisiteEventHandle, the snapshot and a copy of entries  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:445-450  :: ctx->submittedAt=now; ctx->prerequisiteEventHandle=eventHandle (0 when overload without event is used).
  * Resolves per-entry mrKey from client memory registry, discarding any caller-supplied mrKey  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:451-459  :: entry.mrKey.reset(); lookup registeredRegions_ by entry.buffer.handle; hit -> entry.mrKey = RegisteredMemory.tokenId; miss -> remains nullopt (fails later with BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED).
  * Initializes caller-order entryStatus with N OK entries  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:460  :: ctx->entryStatus.assign(entries.size(), Status::OK()); this vector is later indexed by originalIndices.
  * Registers the task in ClientTaskManager and obtains the public TaskId  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:462  :: taskManager_.Submit(std::move(ctx), taskId); TaskManagerBase assigns a monotonic id (skipping 0) and sets state PENDING (task_manager_base.h:55-73).

--- PHASE: P2 Client WorkerLoop dequeue and eventHandle synchronization  [thread: KvClientImpl::WorkerLoop (single thread)] ---
  * Init creates exactly one client worker thread and the SPSC queue depth  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:177-180  :: queueDepth=max(2,maxInflightTasks); taskQueue_.Setup(queueDepth+1); worker_ = std::thread(&KvClientImpl::WorkerLoop).

This blocks all other client tasks while waiting.

--- PHASE: P3 Client routing and per-ASU TransportTask construction  [thread: KvClientImpl::WorkerLoop (client)] ---

--- PHASE: P4 Transport task submission and the two transport threads  [thread: client thread calls Submit; transport WorkerLoop executes] ---
  * AsuTransportImpl::Submit sizes entryStatus from the entries and sets the task deadline  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:231-238  :: IsEntryBatchOp -> entryCount=entries.size(); entryStatus.assign(entryCount, OK); deadline = TaskDeadline(config_.timeoutMs) (0 -> time_point::max, kv_transport_impl.cc:42-46).

--- PHASE: P5 IoScheduler split of the batch path (batchLoadIoNum/batchStoreIoNum + server capability clamp)  [thread: transport WorkerLoop] ---

Logs effective limits.

  * Configured io nums come from transport.* keys shared by every transport config  @ kv_semantics/src/common/utils/parser_common.cc:192-207  :: batchLoadIoNum/batch_load_io_num etc.; config_utils.cc:213-247 applies the same transportFields to every config.transportConfigs entry and then merges asuInfo endpoints.

--- PHASE: P6 Per-sub-batch BatchStore/BatchRetrieve SQE construction (36B entries)  [thread: transport WorkerLoop] ---
  * Each sub-batch gets its own entryStatus sized to the slice  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:351-356  :: entryStatus.assign(subBatch.entries.size, Status::OK()) inside BuildEntrySubBatchRequest.
  * Op type is translated to the wire opcode  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.h:41-53  :: BATCH_STORE -> KvOpcode::BatchStore (0x45), BATCH_LOAD -> KvOpcode::BatchRetrieve (0x46), LOAD -> 0x2 Retrieve, STORE -> 0x1 Store (kv_protocol.h:37-45).
  * All entries in the slice must carry a resolved mrKey  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:45-55,359-363  :: ValidateSqeMrKeys; missing -> StatusCode::BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED "entry buffer is not registered, entryIndex=i"; SetSubBatchBuildFailed marks the sub-batch COMPLETED and aborts the whole transport task before send.
  * Allocates a 16-bit cid for the sub-batch  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:279-284  :: nextRequestCid_.fetch_add(1); if result 0, allocate again, so cid is never 0.
  * Allocates the per-sub-batch flag/CQE response buffer  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:43,72-99,125-137  :: kFlagBufferHeaderSize=16; size = 16 + (batchNum+1)/2 bytes for BatchStore/BatchRetrieve (4 bits per entry), 16+(N+7)/8 for Delete/Exist (1 bit), 16+1 for Store/Retrieve/KeepAlive. PrepareSubBatchRequest sets opType/cid then AllocateSubBatchFlagBuffer (failure -> INVALID_ARGUMENT/RESOURCE_BUSY and sub-batch failed).
  * Builds KvBatchStoreRequest / KvBatchRetrieveRequest from config attrs and flag buffer  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:169-194,232-255  :: kv_ns_id/dtype/dspec/lr from config_.attrs (missing -> 0/false); response_buffer_addr=flagBuffer.device_addr; response_mr_key=flagBuffer.tokenId; rflag=true; batch_number=slice size; per entry: key, offset, buffer_addr=entry.buffer.region.addr, mr_key=*entry.mrKey, length=entry.buffer.region.size.
  * Packed size is 64B header + 36B per entry  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:434-438,635-639  :: (kSqeDwordCount(16) + batch_number*kBatchEntryDwordCount(9)) * 4 bytes = 64 + 36*N; constants at kv_protocol.h:49-58 (kBatchEntrySizeBytes=36, kMaxBatchNumber=110).
  * Packs the batch SQE header  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:440-463,641-663  :: d0 = cid<<16 | fixed 0x3<<14 | opcode | rflag<<13; d1=kv_ns_id; d2=dtype<<13|dspec<<8 (BatchStore only); d3/d4=response_buffer_addr lo/hi; d5=response_mr_key; d8=batch_number*36; d9=DptrType::Batch(0x1)<<24; d10=batch_number; d11 bit31=lr; entries follow at dword 16.
  * Packs each 36-byte entry (9 dwords)  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:548-559,738-749  :: +0 offset(u32); +4 8-byte key; +12 dwords 3-4 reserved zero; +20 buffer_addr lo, +24 buffer_addr hi; +28 length[23:0] | mr_key[7:0]<<24; +32 mr_key[31:8] | DptrType::Standard(0x40)<<24.
  * Validates the whole request before packing  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:465-546,665-736  :: batch_number in [1,110] and == entries.size(); response addr/key non-zero when rflag; 512B alignment of offset and length; non-zero key/buffer_addr/length; length <= 24 bits; dtype <= 0x7; dspec <= 0x1F.
  * Allocates a send-buffer slot and serializes the SQE through ProtocolManager  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:139-167  :: GetPackedSize -> sendBufferManager_.Allocate(packedSize, sendSge) (slot size default 4160, kv_types.h:191) -> PackRequest -> proto->PackSqe; any failure calls SetSubBatchBuildFailed.
  * ProtocolManager dispatches by opcode  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:1201-1226  :: registry maps 0x45 -> KvBatchStoreProtocol, 0x46 -> KvBatchRetrieveProtocol, etc.; GetPackedSize/PackRequest delegate.

--- PHASE: P7 Connections, send call, and synchronous finalize check  [thread: transport WorkerLoop] ---
  * Assigns one connection channel per sub-batch  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:286-305  :: connManager_->SelectConnection() per context; null -> kv_transport_task_connection_errors_total++, sub-batch entryStatus filled CONNECTION_ERROR, context COMPLETED, and the whole Execute aborts (returns non-OK).
  * SelectConnection round-robin/least-loaded increments per-channel inflight  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/conn/connection_manager.cc:118-134,265-335  :: Skips non-ACTIVE and channels at maxInflightPerChannel_ (min(256, ioQueueDepth)); IncrementInflight on selection; ReleaseInflight happens in ReleaseSubBatchResources.
  * Builds one TransProvider::SendIoBatch per sub-batch  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:115-128  :: {channel->GetConnection(), sendSge.device_addr, flagBuffer.device_addr, sendSge.length}.
  * Issues ONE provider Send call for all sub-batches of this transport task  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:130-167  :: kernel_count/quiet_count from config attrs; transProvider_->Send(ioBatches,...); returned status count mismatch -> every sub-batch INTERNAL_ERROR + release; per-slice failure -> SetSubBatchSendFailed + connManager_->ReportFailure + release.
  * Records pre-send and send metrics and notifies the client send-completion hook  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:328-350  :: kv_transport_task_pre_send_duration_seconds/queue/process; SendSubBatchBuffers; sendCompletedAt; sendReturned=true; kv_transport_task_send_duration_seconds + per-node asuSendMetric_; task->onSendComplete().
  * Publishes sub-batch contexts into the task, initializes the pending count and tries to finalize  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:352-375  :: Under task->mutex: if already canceled -> release all and return false; if pre-send failed -> AbortSubBatchesBeforeSend; *task->subBatchContexts = std::move(contexts); InitializeRemainingSubBatchCount(); TryFinalizeFromSubBatches(); returns task->Done().

--- PHASE: P8 CompletionLoop: CQE poll, 4-bit result decode, per-entry status  [thread: AsuTransportImpl::CompletionLoop (transport)] ---
  * Poll only operates on INFLIGHT tasks that own sub-batches  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:379-389  :: state != INFLIGHT or empty subBatchContexts -> return false.
  * Deadline branch fails the entire task with TIMEOUT  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:391-405  :: kv_transport_task_completion_timeouts_total++; fill task.entryStatus and every pending sub-batch entryStatus with TIMEOUT; connManager_->ReportFailure(channel); CompleteSubBatch for each; finalStatus=TIMEOUT; state=COMPLETED.
  * Reads the CQE cid from the flag buffer and matches it to the sub-batch cid  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:407-425  :: responseData = flagBuffer.local_addr; ProtocolManager::PollResponseCid loads dword 3 with acquire and masks 0xFFFF (kv_protocol.cc:1239-1244); cid==0 or != subBatchContext.cid -> continue (still in flight).
  * Unpacks the response using the sub-batch entry count as batch_number  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:427-435  :: batchNumber = subBatchContext.entryStatus.size(); UnpackResponse(responseData, ToKvOpcode(opType), batchNumber, response); failure -> all entryStatus of the sub-batch = that status and complete.
  * Decodes the CQE header: cid = dword3[15:0], status = dword3[31:17]  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:30-34  :: UnpackCqeBase used by every protocol.
  * Decodes the 4-bit-per-entry result buffer starting at dword 4 (byte 16)  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:36-45,561-567,751-757  :: result_buffer.resize(batch_number); for entry i: dword=i/8, shift=(i%8)*4, value=(result_data[dword]>>shift)&0xF. BatchStore/BatchRetrieve UnpackCqe call UnpackResultBuffer4Bit(data + kCqeDwordCount, batch_number).
  * Maps the 15-bit CQE status word to StatusCode  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:104-137  :: 0 -> OK; otherwise 0x10000|raw mapped to CQE_* (e.g. 0x732 CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER, 0x716 CQE_IO_TIMEOUT, 0x706 CQE_INTERNAL_ERROR); unknown -> IO_ERROR.
  * Fills per-entry statuses from the response  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:139-167  :: status OK -> all entries OK (non-query); status != CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER or empty result_buffer -> every entry gets the sub-batch status; otherwise entry i = ResultBufferEntryToStatus(opType, result_buffer[i]).
  * Decodes each 4-bit entry nibble into an entry StatusCode  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:36-100  :: BATCH_STORE/BATCH_LOAD: raw 0 -> OK, 0x101 retry advised, 0x102 no-retry, 0x103 key not found, 0x104 data not exist, else IO_ERROR; DELETE uses 0x20x; QUERY uses 0x30x.
  * Records channel health from the CQE status  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:437-455  :: KvResponseStatusToSubBatchStatus(response.status); query+CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER is treated as a successful sub-batch (440-445); CQE_INTERNAL_ERROR/CQE_IO_TIMEOUT -> ReportFailure (io timeout metric); otherwise ReportSuccess; then CompleteSubBatch(task, ctx, status).
  * Per-sub-batch completion decrements the task's remaining counter  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:248-256  :: ReleaseSubBatchResources (send slot, flag slot, channel inflight), state=COMPLETED, status=status, --task.remainingSubBatchCount.
  * Finalizes only when the pending count reaches zero  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:32-63  :: InitializeRemainingSubBatchCount counts PENDING contexts (43-49); TryFinalizeFromSubBatches sets finalStatus=BuildFinalStatus and state=COMPLETED (51-63); BuildFinalStatus returns PARTIAL_FAILED if any sub-batch status is non-OK, else OK (32-41); empty context list -> PARTIAL_FAILED.

--- PHASE: P9 TransportTaskManager::BuildResult flattening and client callback  [thread: transport CompletionLoop/WorkerLoop -> client] ---
  * NotifyCompletion builds the result once and removes the transport task  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:65-84  :: e2e/completion metrics; BuildResult(*task,result); NotifyCompletion guarded by completionNotified.exchange; Remove(taskId).
  * BuildResult seeds the result from the task then flattens sub-batch entryStatus in vector order  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:86-104  :: result.status=task.finalStatus; result.entryStatus=task.entryStatus (size = all entries of the transport task); then resultIndex walks subBatchContexts 0..k-1 and copies each subBatchContext.entryStatus in order (90-98). Order = IoScheduler slice order = this transport task's entry order; it is NOT caller order.
  * The flattened result is delivered through the client-installed onComplete callback  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:325-329  :: onComplete -> ClientTaskManager::CompleteTransportTask(task, taskIndex, result).
  * Client maps each transport-local entry index back to the caller index with originalIndices  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:196-200  :: task->entryStatus[originalIndices[i]] = (i < result.entryStatus.size() ? result.entryStatus[i] : completionStatus); this restores exact caller order across ASAs.
  * Counts down remaining transport tasks and finalizes on the last one  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:202-204  :: remainingTransportTasks.fetch_sub(1)==1 -> Finalize(task) under the same waitMu lock.
  * Finalize aggregates transport statuses into the client status  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:230-264  :: Logs each failed transport task; finalStatus = OK only if no transport task failed, else PARTIAL_FAILED; completion metric; state=COMPLETED; cv.notify_all().
  * Wait returns the caller-order result and drops the task  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:301-319  :: taskManager_.Wait -> WaitContext (cv wait) -> BuildResult -> Remove; wait metrics; refresh if the status suggests a stale view.
  * ClientTaskManager::BuildResult exposes entryStatus and queryResult  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:352-364  :: result.entryStatus = task->entryStatus (already indexed by caller order); queryResult only for QUERY, reset otherwise; in-progress -> IN_PROGRESS.
  * Wait timeout is reported without discarding partial entry statuses  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:366-384  :: cv.wait_for(timeoutMs); BuildResult always called; if not done result.status=TIMEOUT with client_task_id/op/wait_ms.

--- PHASE: P10 Single-entry LoadAsync/StoreAsync contrast  [thread: same pool/worker threads, different op type and SQE] ---
  * StoreAsync/LoadAsync share the same SubmitAsync and differ only by op type  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:252-270  :: AsuOpType::LOAD / AsuOpType::STORE; metrics kv_client_load_*/kv_client_store_*; StoreAsync also has an eventHandle overload, LoadAsync has none.
  * GetSqeIoNum forces one entry per sub-batch  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/io_scheduler.cc:82  :: LOAD/STORE -> 1, therefore N entries produce N sub-batches, N cids and N SQEs instead of ceil(N/batchIoNum).
  * Builds standard Store/Retrieve requests with no batch fields  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:196-230,308-347  :: BuildSqeRequest requires entries->size == 1 for Store/Retrieve; KvStoreRequest/KvRetrieveRequest carry no batch_number, no response_buffer_addr/mr_key and leave rflag=false.
  * Standard SQE pack is a fixed 64 bytes  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:162-181,308-332  :: buffer_addr in d6/d7, mr_key+buffer_length in d8/d9, offset in d10, lr+length in d11, 8-byte key in d12/d13; PackedSize = 16 dwords (kv_protocol.h:196-199,211-214).
  * Flag buffer is still allocated and polled even for single-entry ops  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:79  :: GetFlagBufferSize default -> 16+1 = 17 bytes; BuildSubBatchSendBuffers always passes flagBuffer.device_addr to the provider (kv_submit_flow.cc:122-126) and Poll always reads the CQE from it (trans_task_executor.cc:416-418). Only the base 16B CQE header is expected because rflag is false/response fields are zero.
  * Single-entry CQE decoding ignores batch_number and the result buffer  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:232-236,362-366  :: KvStoreProtocol::UnpackCqe / KvRetrieveProtocol::UnpackCqe only call UnpackCqeBase; entry status therefore comes solely from the CQE status via the empty-result-buffer path in FillEntryStatusFromCqeResult (trans_task_utils.cc:159-161).

BRANCHES:
  ? BatchStoreAsync called with a non-zero eventHandle -> eventHandle is stored as ClientTask::prerequisiteEventHandle and the client WorkerLoop calls runtime::SynchronizeEvent before routing; the task waits (and blocks the single client worker) until the device event completes  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:279-285,448,559-561
  ? BatchStoreAsync called without eventHandle (or with 0) -> prerequisiteEventHandle==0, SynchronizeEvent is skipped, routing proceeds immediately  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:287-290,448,559
  ? BatchLoadAsync called -> Same pipeline with AsuOpType::BATCH_LOAD; no eventHandle overload exists, so no prerequisite synchronization is possible  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:272-277
  ? Event synchronization or device setup fails -> CompleteWithError fills every entryStatus with that status and completes the task; no TransportTask is created  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:558-566
  ? Snapshot missing or snapshot->transports empty at submit -> StatusCode::NOT_INITIALIZED, taskId=kInvalidTaskId  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:432-436
  ? Client task queue is full or the worker has stopped -> RESOURCE_BUSY (queue full) or NOT_INITIALIZED (stopped); the task is removed from ClientTaskManager and taskId reset  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:471-486
  ? buffer.handle was never registered (registeredRegions_ miss) -> entry.mrKey stays nullopt and the transport rejects the sub-batch with BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED before any send  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:451-459
  ? Router returns zero routes (empty entries or no key maps to a node) -> DispatchTask finalizes immediately as OK with the initial all-OK entryStatus; no transport I/O is performed  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:313-318
  ? A routed node has no transport in the snapshot -> BuildTransportTasks fails with NOT_FOUND (nodeId context) and CompleteWithError completes the whole client task  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:276-282,141-144
  ? transport->Submit fails for transport task k -> CompleteUndispatchedTransportTasks: task k gets the dispatch status, later tasks get CANCELED; DispatchTask returns the dispatch error and Process skips the process-duration metric  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:341-347,207-228
  ? ioNum (batchLoadIoNum/batchStoreIoNum) is 0 -> GetSubBatchCount divides by ioNum -> division by zero/UB; there is no config validation for a zero io num  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/io_scheduler.cc:31-34,44,80-90
  ? Server advertises a non-zero batchStoreKeys/batchLoadKeys -> effective io num = min(configured value, advertised value), applied per connection group during transport Init before IoScheduler is constructed  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:117-142
  ? Server advertises 0 (not advertised) or GetServerCapabilities returns UNSUPPORTED -> No clamping; configured batchLoadIoNum/batchStoreIoNum are used as-is (default 110)  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:130-138; kv_semantics/src/trans/conn/connection_manager.cc:55-74
  ? A sliced sub-batch exceeds kMaxBatchNumber (110) -> KvBatchStore/KvBatchRetrieve ValidateRequest returns INVALID_ARGUMENT and the whole transport task is aborted before send; this happens if io num is configured >110 and no server cap lowers it  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:465-477,665-677
  ? Flag-buffer slot size is smaller than 16 + ceil(batch_number/2) or no slot is free -> BufferManager::Allocate returns INVALID_ARGUMENT/RESOURCE_BUSY, SetSubBatchBuildFailed marks the sub-batch complete, and the pre-send abort path runs  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:83-99,125-137
  ? SQE validation fails (misaligned offset/length, zero key/addr/length, dtype>7, dspec>0x1F, rflag inconsistency) -> PackSqe returns INVALID_ARGUMENT -> PackSubBatchRequest fails -> whole transport task aborted before send  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:465-546,665-736
  ? No ACTIVE connection channel is available for a sub-batch -> kv_transport_task_connection_errors_total++, that sub-batch gets CONNECTION_ERROR and is marked COMPLETED; Execute aborts the remaining sub-batches  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:286-305
  ? transProvider_->Send returns a status count != number of sub-batches -> All sub-batches are marked INTERNAL_ERROR and their resources are released  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:143-153
  ? A single sub-batch Send returns a failure status -> Only that sub-batch gets the failure (entryStatus filled, COMPLETED, resources released, channel ReportFailure); the other sub-batches continue to completion polling  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:155-166
  ? A sub-batch build/connection/send failure occurs before any send -> AbortSubBatchesBeforeSend releases all contexts, marks the failed one with its status and only contexts after the first failure CANCELED; earlier built-but-never-sent contexts keep OK and their entryStatus stays OK  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:227-246,363
  ? Task deadline (config.timeoutMs, default 100ms) expires in Poll -> Whole transport task gets TIMEOUT: all pending sub-batches filled TIMEOUT, channels ReportFailure, task finalStatus=TIMEOUT, even if some sub-batches already completed OK  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:391-405
  ? PollResponseCid returns cid 0 or a cid != the sub-batch cid -> The sub-batch is skipped for this poll pass and stays PENDING  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:416-425
  ? CQE status is 0 (success) -> FillEntryStatusFromCqeResult fills every entry of the sub-batch with OK (no result-buffer decoding needed)  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:146-149
  ? CQE status is CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER (0x732) and the result buffer is non-empty -> Each entry i is decoded from result_buffer[i] (4-bit nibble) into ENTRY_* / OK statuses; the sub-batch status itself remains 0x732 for BATCH_STORE/BATCH_LOAD  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:159-166
  ? CQE status is non-zero and not CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER, or the result buffer is empty -> Every entry of the sub-batch receives that same status  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:159-161
  ? CQE status is CQE_INTERNAL_ERROR or CQE_IO_TIMEOUT -> connManager_->ReportFailure(channel) is called (plus kv_transport_task_io_timeouts_total for IO timeout); all other CQE statuses call ReportSuccess  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:446-454
  ? Any sub-batch status is non-OK at finalization -> TransportTask BuildFinalStatus returns PARTIAL_FAILED, so the client finalStatus is PARTIAL_FAILED even when all entryStatus values are precise per-entry codes  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:32-41,230-256
  ? Wait timeout elapses before the client task is COMPLETED -> result.status=TIMEOUT while entryStatus is still whatever has been filled so far; the task is removed from the manager  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:366-384; kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:301-319

ERROR PATHS:
  ! SubmitAsync: no snapshot/transports -> NOT_INITIALIZED (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:432-436)
  ! SubmitAsync: opType not LOAD/STORE/BATCH_LOAD/BATCH_STORE -> INVALID_ARGUMENT (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:438-443)
  ! ClientTaskManager::Submit failure (null ctx) -> INVALID_ARGUMENT (kv_semantics/src/common/utils/task_manager_base.h:45-52)
  ! Task disappeared after submit -> INTERNAL_ERROR + taskId reset (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:465-469)
  ! Client worker stopped -> NOT_INITIALIZED, task removed (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:473-478)
  ! Client SPSC queue full -> RESOURCE_BUSY, task removed (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:481-486)
  ! Event synchronization failure -> CompleteWithError, all entryStatus = error (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:558-566)
  ! BuildTransportTasks: snapshot/router/transports missing -> NOT_INITIALIZED (kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:266-271)
  ! BuildTransportTasks: routed ASU has no transport -> NOT_FOUND (kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:276-282)
  ! Transport dispatch failure -> dispatch error for the first undispatched task, CANCELED for the rest (kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:341-347,207-228)
  ! Transport Submit: null task -> INVALID_ARGUMENT (kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:233)
  ! Transport Submit: worker stopped -> NOT_INITIALIZED, taskId reset (kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:252-256)
  ! Transport execute queue full -> RESOURCE_BUSY, task removed (kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:269-273)
  ! PrepareTaskSubBatches: empty entry batch -> INVALID_ARGUMENT (kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:59-62)
  ! ValidateSqeMrKeys: unregistered buffer -> BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED (kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:45-55,359-363)
  ! Flag buffer allocation failure -> INVALID_ARGUMENT or RESOURCE_BUSY (kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:83-99; buffer_manager.cc:166-179)
  ! Send buffer allocation failure -> INVALID_ARGUMENT or RESOURCE_BUSY (kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:143-153)
  ! SQE validation failure -> INVALID_ARGUMENT, whole transport task aborted (kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:465-546,665-736)
  ! No connection channel -> CONNECTION_ERROR per sub-batch (kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:289-300)
  ! Send status-count mismatch -> INTERNAL_ERROR for all sub-batches (kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:143-153)
  ! Per-sub-batch send failure -> that sub-batch gets the provider status, channel ReportFailure (kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:155-166)
  ! UnpackResponse failure -> sub-batch entryStatus all set to that status and completed (kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:427-435)
  ! CQE non-zero status -> mapped CQE_* code; CQE_INTERNAL_ERROR/CQE_IO_TIMEOUT additionally ReportFailure (kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:104-137; trans_task_executor.cc:437-455)
  ! Transport deadline -> TIMEOUT for the whole task (kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:391-405)
  ! Client complete: query result missing/size mismatch -> INTERNAL_ERROR (not reachable for batch ops) (kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:177-191)
  ! Client wait timeout -> TIMEOUT in result.status while entryStatus is partial (kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:366-384)
  ! Shutdown drain timeout -> Drain returns the timeout status; in-flight transport tasks are canceled (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:185-241; task_manager.cc:118-132)

GOTCHAS:
  # The SplitForAsu granularity is per TransportTask (per ASU), not per client call: a caller batch is first routed into one transport task per node, and each transport task is then sliced by batchLoadIoNum/batchStoreIoNum. batch_number in each SQE is that slice size, never the client batch size.
  # Server capability clamping is one-way and per group: effective io num = min(configured, every non-zero advertised batchStoreKeys/batchLoadKeys). A capability of 0 means 'not advertised' and does not clamp. Clamping happens once in AsuTransportImpl::Init before IoScheduler is constructed, so later view refreshes that rebuild transports re-run the clamp against the new connection group capabilities.
  # The protocol hard cap is kMaxBatchNumber = 110 (kv_protocol.h:55) while config defaults are also 110. Configuring batchStoreIoNum/batchLoadIoNum above 110 without a server cap makes ValidateRequest reject every over-110 slice with INVALID_ARGUMENT and abort the entire transport task (not just that slice).
  # flagBufferSlotSize default 71 is exactly 16 + ceil(110/2). Raising the io num above 110 (or lowering flagBufferSlotSize) makes Allocate fail with 'size exceeds slot_capacity', which aborts the task before send.
  # The 4-bit result buffer only occupies 16 + ceil(N/2) bytes; the CQE base header is 4 dwords (16B) and result decoding starts at dword 4. Nibble order is entry i at dword i/8, shift (i%8)*4, i.e. entry 0 is the low nibble of the first result dword.
  # A batch with per-entry errors does NOT surface a per-entry error only: the transport sub-batch status stays CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER (0x732), so TransportTask::BuildFinalStatus -> ClientTaskManager::Finalize report PARTIAL_FAILED at task level. Callers must read TaskResult::entryStatus (ENTRY_* codes 0x101..0x104) to learn which entries failed.
  # For CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER, entryStatus is only decoded when response.result_buffer is non-empty; otherwise every entry is filled with the 0x732 status. The fake backend writes the result buffer only when not all entries are OK, so a zeroed buffer would decode as all-OK if it were ever used.
  # TransportTaskManager::BuildResult concatenates subBatchContexts in vector order, which equals IoScheduler slice order and therefore the transport task's local entry order (ascending original caller indices per node). Caller order is recovered only later by ClientTaskManager::CompleteTransportTask indexing task->entryStatus[originalIndices[i]] (task_manager.cc:196-200).
  # If a transport task is aborted before send because sub-batch k failed to build/connect, contexts 0..k-1 were built but never sent and keep status OK; AbortSubBatchesBeforeSend only overwrites contexts after the first failed one with CANCELED (trans_task_executor.cc:227-246). After flattening, entries of those never-sent sub-batches can appear OK in the result.
  # The eventHandle wait is executed inline on the single client WorkerLoop thread (kv_client_impl.cc:548-569), so a slow device event causes head-of-line blocking for every queued client task. queue-duration metric is measured before the wait, but e2e duration includes it.
  # BatchLoadAsync has no eventHandle overload (kv_client.h:46) while BatchStoreAsync has one (kv_client.h:48-49); the no-event BatchStore overload simply forwards eventHandle=0 and SynchronizeEvent is skipped entirely.
  # AsuTransportImpl::Submit re-assigns task->entryStatus with all-OK values and overwrites any status the client may have set (kv_transport_impl.cc:234-235); the transport owns entry status from that point until BuildResult.
  # For LOAD/STORE (io num = 1) the scheduler still produces N sub-batches with N cids and N flag buffers; each SQE is the fixed 64B Store/Retrieve form (no batch_number, no response buffer, rflag=false). A caller passing N entries to StoreAsync therefore issues N separate SQEs, unlike BatchStoreAsync which issues ceil(N/ioNum) SQEs.
  # The transport always allocates a flag buffer and always polls it for the CQE, even for single-entry Store/Retrieve whose SQE carries rflag=false and zero response fields; only the 16B CQE header is expected there, and UnpackCqe ignores batch_number and result_buffer for those opcodes.
  # GetSubBatchCount computes 1 + (total-1)/ioNum with no zero check, so a configured batchLoadIoNum/batchStoreIoNum of 0 causes a division by zero; there is no parser validation for these fields (parser_common.cc:192-207).
  # All sub-batches of one transport task are sent with a single transProvider_->Send(ioBatches) call, but each sub-batch has its own cid and flag buffer; completion is per sub-batch in Poll, and one poll pass processes at most one CQE per sub-batch.
  # Transport deadline is task-wide (config.timeoutMs, default 100ms). When it fires it overwrites finalStatus with TIMEOUT and marks every still-pending sub-batch TIMEOUT, discarding the fact that earlier sub-batches may have completed successfully. timeoutMs==0 means no deadline.
  # The client queue silently succeeds (OK, all entries OK) when routing produces no transport tasks, e.g. an empty entries vector or keys that route to kInvalidNodeId; no I/O or error is reported (task_manager.cc:313-318).
  # After Wait the client task is removed from ClientTaskManager, so a later Check(taskId) returns true (task not found is treated as done) and a second Wait returns TASK_NOT_FOUND (task_manager.cc:102-116).
  # kv-test's docs/guide/kv-test.md claims store/batch-store both use StoreAsync, but the code maps STORE/RETRIEVE commands to SINGLE_ENTRY_PER_CALL (StoreAsync/LoadAsync per entry) and BATCH_STORE/BATCH_RETRIEVE to ALL_ENTRIES_IN_ONE_CALL (BatchStoreAsync/BatchLoadAsync) (kv_test_app.cc:566-568,629-631).
  # BuildSqeRequest can return a null unique_ptr for an unsupported opcode and is dereferenced without a null check in BuildEntrySubBatchRequest/SubmitKeySubBatchRequest (sqe_request.cc:370-373,388-391); current op-type mapping makes this unreachable, but the guard is absent.

==========================================================================================

ERROR PATHS:
  ! Not initialized: RegisterRegionsOnce -> NotInitialized() at kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:335 (GetSnapshot null at 334; GetSnapshot returns null whenever initialized_ is false).
  ! Business-memory provider missing: NOT_INITIALIZED at kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:342-345 (memoryProvider_ reset by Shutdown at 215).
  ! RegisterMemory failure: PARTIAL_FAILED at kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:359-366; cleanup via UnregisterProviderRegions(memoryProvider_, mrHandles) at 361; needRefresh derived from the same status at 360 and consumed by RegisterRegions at 326.
  ! Handle-count mismatch after RegisterMemory: PARTIAL_FAILED at kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:367-373 with cleanup at 368.
  ! GetMemTokenId failure: PARTIAL_FAILED at kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:377-385 with cleanup at 380; all canonical handles are unregistered, not just the failing one.
  ! Provider bind failure: PARTIAL_FAILED at kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:399-411 after rollback at 402-407; the rollback itself is best-effort — cleanup errors are discarded via (void), so a failed rollback is invisible except through the provider's own state.
  ! BindProviderRegions internal errors: UNSUPPORTED from the base TransProvider::BindMemory (kv_semantics/src/trans_provider/trans_provider.h:69-75) propagates as a bind failure; count mismatch -> INTERNAL_ERROR (kv_client_impl.cc:767-769) or the provider's status (770); token mismatch -> INTERNAL_ERROR (779-781); each path first calls UnregisterProviderRegions at 765/777.
  ! BuildTransport bind failure shuts the new transport down and fails the snapshot: kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:725-730, surfaced through BuildSnapshot at 646-650 and from Init at 169-175.
  ! UnregisterRegions provider failure: first failing status returned with suffix 'handle_count=N' at kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:596-600 and 621-623; the handle remains in registeredRegions_ (612-617), so mrKey still resolves for a registration that may no longer exist at that provider.
  ! UnregisterProviderRegions aggregation: result-count mismatch or any non-OK element becomes the returned failure (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:801-814); empty handle list short-circuits OK at 789.
  ! Unregistered buffer at the transport: ValidateSqeMrKeys returns BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED 'entry buffer is not registered, entryIndex=N' (kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:45-55), consumed by BuildEntrySubBatchRequest at kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:359-362; the sub-batch is marked failed and the client task finishes as PARTIAL_FAILED via TransportTask::BuildFinalStatus (kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:32-41).
  ! SubmitAsync rejections unrelated to memory but on the same path: NOT_INITIALIZED when snapshot/router/transports missing (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:432-436), INVALID_ARGUMENT for non-load/store op types (438-443), RESOURCE_BUSY when the client queue is full (481-485), NOT_INITIALIZED when the worker is stopping (474-478).

GOTCHAS:
  # registeredRegions_ is keyed by the canonical MRHandle returned by memoryProvider_->RegisterMemory (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:357-390, 423), never by per-provider handles; per-provider handles live only in ProviderMemoryState.regionHandles (client/kv_client_impl.h:110-113).
  # memoryProvider_ is built from config.transportConfigs.front() (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:155-166), not from the first ASU in the view. In INDEPENDENT mode this means the ASU matching the front config gets two providers — the memory provider (providerMemoryStates_[0]) and its own transport provider. Test joint order {30,20,10,10} at kv_semantics/tests/client/kv_client_impl_test.cc:1615 reflects this.
  # RegisterRegions is a silent no-op returning OK, with an empty out-vector and no bookkeeping, when snapshot->transports.empty() (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:338). Regions registered before any ASU exists are lost rather than deferred; they will not be bound when a view refresh later adds ASUs.
  # The caller's KVBuffer.mrKey is never trusted: it is reset and recomputed on every SubmitAsync (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:454-457). A handle missing from registeredRegions_ is not an error at submit time (no BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED until the transport builds the SQE, kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:48-52).
  # No in-flight guard exists between UnregisterRegions and queued tasks. mrKey is resolved once at submit time (kv_client_impl.cc:451-459), but the transport later uses the buffer address in the SQE; memoryMu_ does not cover transport execution or the worker thread. The contract is documented only as a header comment (kv_semantics/include/kv_client.h:58-59): handles referenced by queued or in-flight tasks must stay registered.
  # UnregisterRegions has no dedup: duplicate handles in the input produce duplicate UnregisterMemoryDesc entries in one provider batch (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:578-583, 795-799). Handles not present in registeredRegions_ are silently ignored instead of returning an error (582).
  # Partial-failure semantics are asymmetric: on a provider failure its whole canonical mapping set is preserved (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:597-600), so the canonical handle survives the 'still mapped?' scan at 612-617 and remains in registeredRegions_; a retry re-sends the full batch, including local handles that may already have been unregistered.
  # Unregister order is strict reverse of providerMemoryStates_ (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:606-608), releasing bound providers before the owner memoryProvider_ at index 0. Shutdown uses the same reverse order (231-240).
  # providerMemoryStates_ is append-only during the client's lifetime (only cleared on Init failure at 172 and moved out on Shutdown at 214); retired transports are kept in retiredTransports_ (kv_client_impl.h:198-199). UnregisterRegions therefore also hits providers of transports no longer in the active snapshot.
  # In SHARED mode BuildTransport returns early after transport Init (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:714-717): no BindMemory and no ProviderMemoryState. The added ASU relies entirely on the shared memoryProvider_ registration. In INDEPENDENT mode each new ASU's provider must BindMemory all registeredRegions_ and return tokenIds equal to the canonical ones (772-782), otherwise the snapshot build fails.
  # RegisterRegionsOnce holds memoryMu_ for the entire multi-provider operation (341-425) and BuildTransport takes the same lock across transport Init + binding (685-739). A slow transport Init therefore blocks RegisterRegions/UnregisterRegions/SubmitAsync, while a slow provider register blocks refresh. Lock order is mutex_ -> memoryMu_ (674-682 then 685; Shutdown 189-196 then 213).
  # Duplicate canonical handles within one RegisterRegions call are not detected: emplace keeps the first map entry in ProviderMemoryState.regionHandles (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:418-420) and in registeredRegions_ (422-424) while the out-vector still contains every region, so output size and cached state can diverge.
  # needRefresh is only computed from the RegisterMemory failure status (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:360); count-mismatch (367) and token-lookup (377) failures never trigger a background refresh even though they may indicate stale provider/transport state.
