TRACE 6: KvClient（kv_semantics）所有 6 个操作（Query/Load/Store/BatchLoad/BatchStore/Delete）共享的三级任务生命周期与结果聚合：caller SubmitAsync -> ClientTask(每 Client 1 个 WorkerLoop) -> 按 ASU 路由的 TransportTask(每 
==========================================================================================
ENTRYPOINTS: QueryAsync(keys,taskId) -> SubmitAsync(AsuOpType::QUERY, keys) : kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:244 | DeleteAsync(keys,taskId) -> SubmitAsync(AsuOpType::DELETE, keys) : kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:292 | LoadAsync(entries,taskId) -> SubmitAsync(AsuOpType::LOAD, entries, eventHandle=0) : kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:252 | StoreAsync(entries,taskId[,eventHandle]) -> SubmitAsync(AsuOpType::STORE, entries, eventHandle) : kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:259,267 | BatchLoadAsync(entries,taskId) -> SubmitAsync(AsuOpType::BATCH_LOAD, entries, 0) : kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:272 | BatchStoreAsync(entries,taskId[,eventHandle]) -> SubmitAsync(AsuOpType::BATCH_STORE, entries, eventHandle) : kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:279,287 | Check(taskId) : kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:299 -> ClientTaskManager::Check kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:102 | Wait(taskId,timeoutMs,result) : kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:301 -> ClientTaskManager::Wait kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:108 | 内部执行入口：KvClientImpl::SubmitAsync(entries) kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:428；SubmitAsync(keys) kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:493 | 内部线程入口：KvClientImpl::WorkerLoop kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:548；AsuTransportImpl::WorkerLoop kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:278；AsuTransportImpl::CompletionLoop kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:286

--- PHASE: 1. 提交（caller thread）：构造 ClientTask 并入队  [thread: 调用方线程] ---
  * 入口按 key/entry 两族分流  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:244-297  :: Query/Delete 走 key 重载；Load/Store/BatchLoad/BatchStore 走 entry 重载。只有 StoreAsync/BatchStoreAsync 有 eventHandle 重载（无该参数时传 0：259-270、279-290）。每次调用后按 op 记录 kv_client_*_requests/entries/errors metrics（RecordSubmit：87-98）。
  * SubmitAsync(entries) 校验并构造 ClientTask  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:428-460  :: 先 GetSnapshot()，要求 snapshot/router/transports 非空，否则 NOT_INITIALIZED 且 taskId=kInvalidTaskId；opType 必须是 LOAD/STORE/BATCH_LOAD/BATCH_STORE，否则 INVALID_ARGUMENT。ctx->submittedAt=now、opType、prerequisiteEventHandle、viewSnapshot；entries 整体拷贝；在 memoryMu_ 下逐条清除调用方传入的 mrKey，再用 registeredRegions_[buffer.handle].tokenId 回填（未注册则保持 nullopt）；entryStatus.assign(size, OK)。
  * SubmitAsync(keys) 校验并构造 ClientTask  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:493-516  :: 同样要求 snapshot/router/transports；opType 必须是 QUERY/DELETE，否则 INVALID_ARGUMENT。ctx->keys=keys；entryStatus.assign(keys.size(), OK)；QUERY 时 queryResult.exists.assign(keys.size(),0)，DELETE 时为空。
  * TaskManagerBase::Submit 分配 TaskId 并落表  @ kv_semantics/src/common/utils/task_manager_base.h:55-73  :: 先 state.store(PENDING)（initialState 来自 ClientTaskManager 构造：kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.h:36）；在 shared_mutex 独占锁内 nextTaskId_.fetch_add(1)（初值 1，105 行），跳过 kInvalidTaskId(0) 或已存在 id（67-68），写 ctx->taskId 并 tasks_.emplace。
  * 入队 taskQueue_  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:462-490  :: Get(taskId) 再确认存在；在 producerMu_ 下检查 stopWorker_（已停则 Remove + taskId=0 + NOT_INITIALIZED）；记录 enqueuedAt；TryPush 失败则 Remove + taskId=0 + RESOURCE_BUSY；成功后 workerCv_.notify_one() 并记录 kv_client_task_enqueue_duration_seconds。
  * 返回给调用方  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:487-490  :: 同步返回 Status::OK 与有效 TaskId 仅表示“已入队”，不代表任何路由/发送/ASU 结果；后续只能通过 Check/Wait 观测。

--- PHASE: 2. 客户端分发（client WorkerLoop）：路由并生成 TransportTask  [thread: KvClientImpl::WorkerLoop（每 Client 单线程）] ---
  * 出队并记录排队时长  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:553-569  :: processTask 先写 processingStartedAt，记录 kv_client_task_queue_duration_seconds；ConsumerLoop 在 stop 后仍有收尾 TryPop 循环（571-572），保证已入队任务在 Shutdown 期间被处理。
  * 等待前置 event  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:558-561  :: device 初始化失败(status 初始值)或 prerequisiteEventHandle!=0 时调用 runtime::SynchronizeEvent；失败则跳过 Process，直接 CompleteWithError(ctx,status)。该路径只有 Store/BatchStore 的 eventHandle 重载会触发。
  * Process 进入 INFLIGHT  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:134-154  :: state.store(INFLIGHT)；先 BuildTransportTasks，失败则 CompleteWithError 并返回该 status；否则 DispatchTask；dispatch 成功时记录 kv_client_task_process_duration_seconds。返回的 status 只被 WorkerLoop 用于 IsRefreshNeeded（kv_client_impl.cc:567），不影响调用方（SubmitAsync 早已返回）。
  * BuildTransportTasks 路由  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:266-310  :: 要求 viewSnapshot/router/transports 存在，否则 NOT_INITIALIZED。QUERY/DELETE 用 task->keys 路由，其余用 entry.key 路由（273-275，ToRouterKeys/ExtractEntryKeys：52-66）。对 routes 中每个 nodeId 校验 snapshot->transports 存在，否则 NOT_FOUND+"nodeId="（276-282）。
  * 按节点切分并搬移数据  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:284-306  :: 每个 nodeId 一个 TransportTask：nodeId、weak_ptr transport、originalIndices；key 族 move keys[idx]，entry 族 move entries[idx]（290-302）。之后把 task->entries/task->keys 清空（305-306），因此调用方 buffer 必须保持有效直到任务完成。
  * 初始化两个倒计数  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:307-308  :: remainingTransportTasks 与 remainingTransportSendTasks 都置为 transportTasks.size()（atomic<size_t> 定义：kv_semantics/src/common/utils/task_context.h:136-137）。前者决定 Finalize 时机，后者只用于 send 时长 metric。
  * DispatchTask 逐节点异步提交  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:312-350  :: transportTasks 为空时在 waitMu 下直接 Finalize 并返回 OK（314-318）。否则按 vector 顺序（顺序来自 unordered_map routes，节点顺序不确定）为每个 TransportTask 设置 onComplete（捕获 weak_ptr<ClientTask> 与 taskIndex）和 onSendComplete，设置 opType，调用 transport->Submit。
  * 分发失败处理  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:341-347  :: 任一 Submit 失败：AddContext(status,"nodeId=") 后调用 CompleteUndispatchedTransportTasks(task, taskIndex, dispatchStatus)，并立即返回 dispatchStatus（但已成功派发的更早 TransportTask 仍在飞行，Finalize 会延后）。

--- PHASE: 3. 传输执行（transport WorkerLoop）：拆子批、建 SQE、发送  [thread: 每个 ASU 的 AsuTransportImpl::WorkerLoop（每 transport 单线程）] ---
  * AsuTransportImpl::Submit 初始化位置  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:231-238  :: 按 op 类型 entryCount=entries.size()（entry 族）或 keys.size()（key 族）重置 task->entryStatus 为全 OK；deadline=TaskDeadline(config_.timeoutMs)（42-46：0 表示 time_point::max 永不过期）。
  * SubmitTask 登记并入 transport 队列  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:250-276  :: producerMu_ 下若 stopWorker_ 或 worker 未启动 -> taskId=kInvalidTaskId + NOT_INITIALIZED；submittedAt=now；TransportTaskManager::Submit 分配 transport 侧 TaskId；executeQueue_.TryPush 失败 -> Remove + RESOURCE_BUSY；成功 notify_one。
  * Execute 取状态所有权  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:307-318  :: state.compare_exchange_strong(PENDING->INFLIGHT)，CAS 失败返回 false（已被取消/重复执行）；记录 asuCompletionMetric 指针，然后 PrepareTaskSubBatches。
  * 按 op 拆子批  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:54-113  :: entry 族：空 entries -> INVALID_ARGUMENT；ioScheduler_.SplitForAsu 后逐块 BuildEntrySubBatchRequest。key 族：空 keys -> INVALID_ARGUMENT；按块 SubmitKeySubBatchRequest。KEEP_ALIVE：固定 1 个子批 SubmitKeepAliveRequest（仅 transport 内部可达，KvClient 无公开 API）。
  * 子批切分粒度  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/io_scheduler.cc:80-90  :: LOAD/STORE 的 ioNum=1（每条 entry 一个 SQE/子批/flag buffer/cid）；BATCH_LOAD=batchLoadIoNum、BATCH_STORE=batchStoreIoNum、DELETE=deleteIoNum、QUERY=queryIoNum（默认见 kv_types.h:196-199）。Init 时还会被服务端能力 min 钳制：kv_transport_impl.cc:130-142。
  * 构造子批请求  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:351-409  :: entry 族先 ValidateSqeMrKeys 校验每条 mrKey 有值（45-55，缺则 BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED）；分配 cid（AllocateRequestCid）、按 opcode/batchNum 分配 flag buffer（AllocateSubBatchFlagBuffer：83-99）、pack SQE 到 send buffer（PackSubBatchRequest：139-167）。EXIST 的 sc 属性写入 subBatchContext.useSeekControl（334-338）。
  * 分配连接  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:286-305  :: 每个子批 connManager_->SelectConnection()；无可用 channel 则该子批 CONNECTION_ERROR + kv_transport_task_connection_errors_total，并立即返回（后续子批仍未分配）。
  * 发送  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:130-167  :: BuildSubBatchSendBuffers 为每个子批生成 SendIoBatch（115-128）；transProvider_->Send 批量发送；返回状态数不匹配 -> 全部 INTERNAL_ERROR；单个失败 -> SetSubBatchSendFailed + ReportFailure 并释放该子批资源（其余继续）。发送返回后 task->sendReturned=true 并回调 onSendComplete（trans_task_executor.cc:339-350）。
  * 接管子批并尝试快速完成  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:352-377  :: 加 task->mutex：若已 Done（被 Cancel）则释放全部子批资源并返回 false；预发送阶段失败则 AbortSubBatchesBeforeSend；把局部 subBatchContexts move 进 task；InitializeRemainingSubBatchCount（只数 PENDING）；TryFinalizeFromSubBatches；返回 Done()。
  * WorkerLoop 完成通知  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:278-284  :: Execute 返回 true 时立即 taskManager_.NotifyCompletion(task)；返回 false 则留给 CompletionLoop 轮询。

--- PHASE: 4. 传输完成（transport CompletionLoop）：轮询 CQE 并按子批聚合  [thread: 每个 ASU 的 AsuTransportImpl::CompletionLoop（每 transport 单线程）] ---
  * 周期扫描活动任务  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:286-313  :: 每轮 taskManager_.GetAll()；空则 sleep 50us；对每个 ctx 调 taskExecutor_->Poll；Poll 返回 true 则 NotifyCompletion。连续无完成轮次达到 completionPollSpinLimit 时 yield（304-311）。
  * Poll 前置判断  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:379-390  :: 加 task->mutex；state 非 INFLIGHT 或 subBatchContexts 为空直接 false。空子批的最终化在 Execute 内完成，这里不再处理。
  * 整体 deadline 超时  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:391-405  :: now>=deadline：所有未完成子批 entryStatus=TIMEOUT、ReportFailure(channel)、CompleteSubBatch(TIMEOUT)；finalStatus=TIMEOUT、state=COMPLETED，kv_transport_task_completion_timeouts_total+1。
  * 逐子批匹配 cid  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:407-425  :: 读取 flagBuffer 中完成的 cid；PollResponseCid 失败或 cid==0 / 与 subBatchContext.cid 不符则本轮跳过。
  * 解包并按 op 计算 entry status  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:427-445  :: UnpackResponse 失败 -> 整个子批 entryStatus=该 status 并 CompleteSubBatch。成功则 subBatchContext.status=KvResponseStatusToSubBatchStatus(response.status)，FillEntryStatusFromCqeResult 逐 entry 映射（trans_task_utils.cc:139-167）。QUERY 且状态为 CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER 时归一化为 OK（443-445），使“部分 key 不存在”不算传输失败。
  * 连接健康反馈  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:446-455  :: CQE_INTERNAL_ERROR / CQE_IO_TIMEOUT -> ReportFailure（IO timeout 另计 kv_transport_task_io_timeouts_total）；其余（含 QUERY 结果缓冲）-> ReportSuccess；随后 CompleteSubBatch。
  * 子批完成扣减  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:248-256  :: CompleteSubBatch：释放 send/flag buffer 槽位与 channel inflight、state=COMPLETED、写入 status（会覆盖上面 QUERY 归一化前的值）、--remainingSubBatchCount。
  * 聚合子批结果  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:51-63  :: TryFinalizeFromSubBatches：subBatchContexts 为空 -> finalStatus=PARTIAL_FAILED("transport task partially failed") 并 COMPLETED；remainingSubBatchCount!=0 -> 保持 INFLIGHT；否则 finalStatus=BuildFinalStatus()（任一子批非 OK 即 PARTIAL_FAILED，32-41）并 COMPLETED。
  * NotifyCompletion 生成 TaskResult  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:65-84  :: 记录 kv_transport_task_e2e/completion/node 维度时长；BuildResult（86-104）：status=finalStatus，entryStatus 先拷 task->entryStatus，再用各子批 entryStatus 依序覆盖（90-98）；QUERY 时 queryResult=BuildQueryResultFromEntryStatus(entryStatus)（100-103）。随后 task->NotifyCompletion(result)（completionNotified CAS 保证最多一次）并 Remove(taskId)。

--- PHASE: 5. 客户端完成聚合（transport 回调线程）：倒计数与 Finalize  [thread: transport CompletionLoop 线程（回调）或 client WorkerLoop（空路由的同步快速路径）] ---
  * CompleteTransportTask 去重  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:168-174  :: 在 task->waitMu 下取 transportTasks[index]；clientCompleted 已 true 则直接返回，避免重复扣减。
  * QUERY 结果校验与回填  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:175-192  :: 仅当 opType==QUERY 且 transport 返回 OK 时：缺 queryResult -> INTERNAL_ERROR；exists.size()!=originalIndices.size() -> INTERNAL_ERROR；否则按 originalIndices 写回 client 顺序的 queryResult.exists。
  * entryStatus 回填  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:194-200  :: transportTask->clientCompleted=true、finalStatus=completionStatus；逐 index 将 result.entryStatus[index] 写入 client 的 originalIndices 位置；缺失时用 completionStatus，QUERY 校验失败时用 completionStatus。
  * 倒计数到 0 触发 Finalize  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:202-204  :: remainingTransportTasks.fetch_sub(acq_rel)==1 表示这是最后一个 -> Finalize(task)。
  * 未派发任务批量收敛  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:207-228  :: CompleteUndispatchedTransportTasks：index==firstTransportTaskIndex 的 TransportTask finalStatus=dispatchStatus，其后全部 CANCELED("transport task not dispatched after a dispatch failure")；clientCompleted=true，entryStatus 回填；每个都 --remainingTransportTasks；全部到 0 时 Finalize。
  * Finalize 生成客户端终态  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:230-264  :: QUERY 先按 exists 前缀重算 prefixHitKeys（232-238，覆盖 transport 侧的值）；统计 finalStatus 非 OK 的 transport task 数并逐条 KV_ERROR；finalStatus = 0 个失败 ? OK : PARTIAL_FAILED("client task partially failed")；RecordClientTaskCompletion（79-88，completionMetricRecorded CAS + enqueuedAt 非 0）；state=COMPLETED；cv.notify_all。
  * 客户端级错误直接完成  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:156-166  :: CompleteWithError（路由构建失败、device/event 失败）：把全部 entryStatus 覆盖为同一 status，finalStatus=status，记录 metric 与 KV_ERROR，state=COMPLETED，cv.notify_all；不走 transport 计数。

--- PHASE: 6. 调用方观测：Check 与 Wait（结果组装）  [thread: 调用方线程] ---
  * Check 语义  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:102-106  :: Get(taskId) 返回空或 task->Done() 都返回 true；未完成返回 false。Check 不会移除任务，也不会给出状态码。
  * Wait 归一化超时参数  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:301-319  :: timeoutMs==0 时改用 config_.defaultWaitTimeoutMs（默认 100ms，kv_types.h:215）；调用 taskManager_.Wait；按 status/result 记录 wait metrics；TASK_NOT_FOUND 直接返回；若 viewServer 认为 status 或 result 需要刷新则 RequestBackgroundRefresh。
  * WaitContext 阻塞  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:366-384  :: task 不存在 -> TASK_NOT_FOUND；在 task->waitMu 上 cv.wait_for(milliseconds(waitTimeoutMs), pred=Done())。
  * BuildResult 快照  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:352-364  :: result.status = Done() ? finalStatus : IN_PROGRESS("client task in progress")；result.entryStatus = task->entryStatus 当前快照；QUERY 附带 task->queryResult，其余 queryResult.reset()。
  * 超时覆盖  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:371-382  :: wait_for 返回 false（未 Done）时把 result.status 覆盖为 TIMEOUT（message 含 client_task_id/op/wait_ms）并 KV_ERROR；返回的 Status 与 result.status 相同。
  * Wait 无条件移除任务  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:108-116  :: Wait 在 WaitContext 之后无论成功、失败还是超时都 (void)Remove(taskId)；因此 Wait 是“取走结果”的一次性操作。Drain 走同一 WaitContext 但自己 Remove（118-132）。
  * 极简成功路径  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:312-318  :: transportTasks 为空（输入为空或所有 key 未路由）时 DispatchTask 直接 Finalize：状态 OK、entryStatus 保持初始全 OK（QUERY exists 全 0、prefixHitKeys=0）。

--- PHASE: 7. Shutdown/Drain 与传输侧取消  [thread: 调用方线程 + client WorkerLoop + transport 两个循环线程] ---
  * 关闭提交闸门  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:185-199  :: initialized_=false、stopWorker_=true、notify；JoinBackgroundRefresh；join client WorkerLoop（其尾部 TryPop 会处理已入队任务，571-572）。此后 SubmitAsync 在 producerMu_ 下返回 NOT_INITIALIZED。
  * Drain 全部客户端任务  @ kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:219-221  :: taskManager_.Drain(defaultWaitTimeoutMs)：对每个未完成任务 WaitContext（超时即放弃）并 Remove，返回第一个非 OK 状态（例如 TIMEOUT）。
  * 关闭 transport  @ kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:168-221  :: stopWorker_ + join worker；若 completionWorker 存在、config_.timeoutMs!=0 且仍有 INFLIGHT 任务则先 sleep timeoutMs；停 completion 线程；对剩余任务 taskExecutor_->Cancel（trans_task_executor.cc:258-277：置 CANCELED、释放资源、COMPLETED）并 NotifyCompletion；最后 Remove 全部并关闭 executor/connManager。
  * 取消后的回调语义  @ kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:168-205  :: 若 ClientTask 仍存活，CANCELED 结果按 TransportTask 回填，Finalize 后客户端仍报 PARTIAL_FAILED；若已被 Wait/Drain 移除且无其他 shared_ptr 持有，回调 lambda 的 weak_ptr.lock() 失败而静默丢弃。

ERROR PATHS:
  ! 同步提交错误（任务不存在）：NOT_INITIALIZED（无 snapshot/transports 或 worker 已停）、INVALID_ARGUMENT（op 与参数族不匹配）、RESOURCE_BUSY（client taskQueue_ 满）、INTERNAL_ERROR（提交后任务立即消失）；均伴随 taskId=kInvalidTaskId，不会进入任务表（kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:432-443,474-485）
  ! 客户端级构建/前置错误（任务已存在但未派发）：BuildTransportTasks 返回 NOT_INITIALIZED / NOT_FOUND(+nodeId)，或 SynchronizeEvent 失败 -> CompleteWithError 把该 status 写入 finalStatus 和每一条 entryStatus，Wait 原样返回（kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:141-145,156-166,266-282）
  ! 分发失败：transport->Submit 返回 CONNECTION_ERROR/RESOURCE_BUSY/NOT_INITIALIZED 等 -> 该节点 entryStatus=dispatchStatus，其后节点 entryStatus=CANCELED，先派发节点按各自结果；Wait 顶层状态统一为 PARTIAL_FAILED（kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:207-228,341-347）
  ! 传输侧预发送失败：空批 INVALID_ARGUMENT/UNSUPPORTED、未注册 buffer BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED、flag/send buffer 分配失败、PackRequest 失败 -> AbortSubBatchesBeforeSend，TransportTask finalStatus=PARTIAL_FAILED（原始码仅在 entryStatus/子批 status），客户端最终 PARTIAL_FAILED（kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:227-246,325-328；kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:32-41,53-57）
  ! 连接不可用：CONNECTION_ERROR 子批 + 后续 CANCELED（kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:289-300）
  ! 发送失败：整批状态数不匹配 -> INTERNAL_ERROR；单子批失败 -> 该子批 Send 错误码 + ReportFailure（kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:143-166）
  ! 响应解析失败：UnpackResponse 错误 -> 该子批所有 entry 使用该 status（kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:427-434）
  ! CQE 业务错误：CQE_KEY_NOT_EXIST / CQE_INTERNAL_ERROR / CQE_IO_TIMEOUT / CQE_RESOURCE_BUSY 等映射为子批 status；ENTRY_* 码（ENTRY_KEY_NOT_FOUND / ENTRY_DELETE_FAILED / ENTRY_RETRY_ADVISED 等）按 op 经 result buffer 映射到每条 entry（kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:36-137,139-167）
  ! 传输超时：deadline 到期 -> 子批 TIMEOUT + ReportFailure + finalStatus=TIMEOUT（kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:391-405）；客户端顶层仍为 PARTIAL_FAILED
  ! 客户端 QUERY 结果校验失败：缺 queryResult 或 exists 长度不符 -> INTERNAL_ERROR（kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:177-191）
  ! Wait 超时：Status/result.status=TIMEOUT，message 含 client_task_id/op/wait_ms；任务被移除但后台继续（kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:375-382,114）
  ! Wait 二次调用 / 等待已超时任务：TASK_NOT_FOUND（kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:108-111）
  ! Shutdown 取消：Cancel 置 CANCELED（entryStatus 与 finalStatus），NotifyCompletion 回调；若 ClientTask 已无强引用则 weak_ptr 失效、结果静默丢弃（kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:258-277；kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:196-200）
  ! backpressure：client queue 深度 = max(2,maxInflightTasks)+1（kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:148-149,177-179）；transport 队列同理（kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:158-159），超限在对应 Submit 立即失败

GOTCHAS:
  # Check 无法区分“已完成”与“不存在”：ClientTaskManager::Check 返回 !task || task->Done()，因此非法 TaskId、已被 Wait/Drain 移除的 id、Shutdown 后的 id 都返回 true（kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:102-106）
  # Wait 是破坏性读：成功、失败、超时三种情况都会 Remove(taskId)（kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:113-115）。超时后任务仍在后台跑，但结果已不可再取；第二次 Wait 一律 TASK_NOT_FOUND（测试 kv_semantics/tests/client/kv_client_impl_test.cc:1959-1986）
  # Wait 超时后若没有其他 shared_ptr 持有 ClientTask，transport 的 onComplete 只持有 weak_ptr，回调 lock 失败即静默丢弃（kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:324-329）；此时调用方若释放/注销 entry buffer，传输侧仍可能读写该内存，属于使用风险
  # 客户端 Finalize 从不上报底层错误码：只要有一个 TransportTask 失败就统一 PARTIAL_FAILED（“client task partially failed”），即使 100% 失败也不是 IO_ERROR/TIMEOUT/CONNECTION_ERROR；原始码只在 entryStatus、transportTask->finalStatus 日志中（kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:240-256）
  # 分发失败的真实状态码只出现在 entryStatus 中，Wait 的顶层 Status 仍是 PARTIAL_FAILED；且哪个节点拿到 dispatchStatus 取决于 router 返回的 unordered_map 迭代顺序，不确定（kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:320-347；测试 1883-1935 断言 CONNECTION_ERROR+CANCELED 分布）
  # 路由器对 kInvalidNodeId（如空环、Maglev 空表、Contiguous 未启用 fullSpread）会静默丢弃 key（kv_semantics/src/client/router/router.cc:140-143），这些 entry 的 entryStatus 保持初始 OK、QUERY exists 保持 0，任务可整体返回 OK——即“未落任何 ASU 也算成功”
  # 预发送阶段部分失败时，失败之前已构建好的子批根本没有 Send，却保留 OK 的 entryStatus（AbortSubBatchesBeforeSend 只把失败之后与未构建部分标 CANCELED）（kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:227-246）
  # QUERY 成功时 entryStatus 里的 ENTRY_KEY_EXIST / ENTRY_KEY_NOT_EXIST 是非 OK 的 StatusCode，但它们是查询结果而非失败；客户端 QueryResult.exists 只由 code==ENTRY_KEY_EXIST 推导（kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:169-180）
  # prefixHitKeys 计算两次：transport BuildResult 里一次（trans_task_utils.cc:169-180），客户端 Finalize 在所有 transport 回填 exists 之后重算并覆盖（kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:232-238）；因此 Wait 超时快照里的 prefixHitKeys 通常是 0
  # QUERY 的“部分 key 不存在”依赖 CQE_CHECK_RESULT_BUFFER 归一化：Poll 判定后传给 CompleteSubBatch 的 status=OK，否则会被 BuildFinalStatus 误判为 PARTIAL_FAILED（kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:437-455,248-256）
  # 空输入/全部 key 未路由：SubmitAsync 会成功，BuildTransportTasks 产生 0 个 TransportTask，DispatchTask 直接 Finalize 为 OK，完全不碰 transport（kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:312-318）；但空 entries/keys 若直接打到 transport API 则 INVALID_ARGUMENT，且空子批路径 finalStatus=PARTIAL_FAILED（kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:53-57）
  # 切分粒度决定 SQE 数：LOAD/STORE 每条 entry 单独一个子批（ioNum=1），N 条 entry = N 个 cid/flag buffer/响应；BATCH_LOAD/BATCH_STORE/DELETE/QUERY 分别按 batchLoadIoNum/batchStoreIoNum/deleteIoNum/queryIoNum 分块，并会被服务端能力值钳制（kv_semantics/src/trans/task/io_scheduler.cc:80-90；kv_semantics/src/trans/kv_transport_impl.cc:130-142）
  # 两个超时是独立的：transport 的 config_.timeoutMs（默认 100ms，0=永不超时）决定子批 TIMEOUT；client Wait 的 timeoutMs==0 会换成 defaultWaitTimeoutMs（默认 100ms）。两者都超时也会先看到 client 的 TIMEOUT，而任务最终状态可能是 PARTIAL_FAILED
  # remainingTransportSendTasks 只在真正执行 Send 后由 onSendComplete 递减（kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:330-339；kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:349）；预发送失败/未派发的任务永远不会递减，“send 时长” metric 会缺失（仅 metric，无功能影响）
  # TransportTask 对预发送失败只给 PARTIAL_FAILED，空子批也只给 PARTIAL_FAILED；底层 INVALID_ARGUMENT/UNSUPPORTED/BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED 仅通过 entryStatus 泄漏给调用方（kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:32-57）
  # TaskId 在两个层级各自独立编号：ClientTaskManager 与 TransportTaskManager 的 nextTaskId_ 都从 1 开始、跳过 0 与已存在 id（kv_semantics/src/common/utils/task_manager_base.h:66-71,105）；同一个数值在两个命名空间没有关系
  # 任务没有任何取消 API（KvClient 只有 Check/Wait/Shutdown）；客户端任务一旦入队只能等它结束或 Wait 超时放弃，Shutdown 时由 transport Cancel 收尾

--- KvClient::BatchStoreAsync (with eventHandle and without) and KvClient::BatchLoadAsync traced end-to-end: KvClientImpl -> ClientTaskManager (one Worker [confidence=high] ---
ERRORS:
  E: P8 step 7 (CQE status mapping) has a wrong numeric example: '0x706 CQE_INTERNAL_ERROR'. kv_semantics/include/kv_types.h:77 defines CQE_INTERNAL_ERROR = 0x10000|0x006, i.e. raw status 0x006; 0x706 is not a defined code. The fake backend agrees (kv_semantics/src/trans_provider/fake/fake_trans_provider.cc:50, kCqeInternalError = 0x006).
  E: P10 step 4 cites the wrong range for KvRetrieveProtocol::PackSqe: kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/kv_protocol.cc:308-332 starts mid-body and runs into ValidateRequest. PackSqe is 302-320; ValidateRequest is 322-360 (Store's PackSqe is correctly cited as 162-181).
  E: P1 step 4 cites kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:68-98 as 'RecordSubmit', conflating two functions: RecordSubmit is 87-98; batch metric names are chosen in GetSubmitMetrics at 50-85 (batch cases 68-77). This matters only for citation precision, not for the described behavior (2 counters on OK, 3 on error at line 97).
  E: P5 step 4 line cite is imprecise: the 110/110 defaults for batchLoadIoNum/batchStoreIoNum are kv_semantics/include/kv_types.h:196-197; lines 198-199 are deleteIoNum=254 and queryIoNum=256, not the batch defaults.
  E: P6 step 6 overgeneralizes the config attributes: dtype/dspec are only read for KvBatchStoreRequest (kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:175-177). KvBatchRetrieveRequest has no dtype/dspec fields and never reads them (sqe_request.cc:232-255), which is consistent with P6 step 8's 'd2 ... (BatchStore only)' but contradicts the 'kv_ns_id/dtype/dspec/lr' wording.
  E: P9 step 4 omits a condition: the reorder is guarded by !invalidQueryResult (kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:196-200); for batch ops the guard is always true, but the step as written is not the actual expression.
  E: Gotcha 'later view refreshes that rebuild transports re-run the clamp against the new connection group capabilities' is overstated. RefreshView/BuildSnapshot reuses the old transport object for any node already present in the old snapshot (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:637-641, 653), and AsuTransportImpl::Init is only called for newly created transports (kv_client_impl.cc:670-740 -> kv_transport_impl.cc:117-142). Reused transports keep their original clamped io nums indefinitely.
  E: Branch 'A sliced sub-batch exceeds kMaxBatchNumber (110) ... ValidateRequest returns INVALID_ARGUMENT' is conditional on flag-buffer capacity. Build order is AllocateSubBatchFlagBuffer (kv_semantics/src/trans/proto/sqe_request.cc:366-368) before PackSubBatchRequest/ValidateRequest (370-373). With the default flagBufferSlotSize=71 (kv_semantics/include/kv_types.h:194) any slice >110 needs 16+ceil(N/2) > 71 bytes, so BufferManager::Allocate fails first with INVALID_ARGUMENT 'size exceeds slot_capacity' (buffer_manager.cc:172-174). The ValidateRequest path is only reachable if flagBufferSlotSize is raised to at least 16+ceil(ioNum/2).
  E: Branch 'CQE status is 0 (success) -> FillEntryStatusFromCqeResult fills every entry of the sub-batch with OK' is only true for non-QUERY ops; for QUERY it fills ENTRY_KEY_EXIST (kv_semantics/src/trans/utils/trans_task_utils.cc:146-148). Likewise P8 step 8 / the branch list omit the query seek-control branch at trans_task_utils.cc:151-157. This does not affect BatchStore/BatchLoad, but the statements are presented as generic decode rules.
MISSING:
  M: Client WorkerLoop device setup is missing from P2: kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:550-552 creates runtime::Device and calls device.Setup(config_.transportConfigs.front().deviceId) once per worker; the captured deviceStatus (558) gates both SynchronizeEvent and taskManager_.Process for every queued task. P2 begins at processTask (553) and only mentions 'device failure' as an outcome.
  M: Wait's timeout default substitution is missing: kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:303 maps timeoutMs==0 to config_.defaultWaitTimeoutMs before taskManager_.Wait, and ClientTaskManager::Wait performs Get -> WaitContext -> Remove (kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:108-116). P9 step 9 describes cv.wait_for(timeoutMs) without the default and without the Remove-after-wait.
  M: CompleteTransportTask's duplicate-completion guard and status record are missing: early return when transportTask->clientCompleted is already true (kv_semantics/src/client/task/task_manager.cc:172-173) and transportTask->finalStatus = completionStatus (195), which Finalize later aggregates (241-253).
  M: TransportTaskManager::BuildResult's bounded flatten is missing: the inner loop breaks when resultIndex reaches result.entryStatus.size(), and only the inner loop breaks, so surplus sub-batch statuses are silently dropped (kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_manager.cc:90-98). P9 step 2 describes a plain copy of every sub-batch entryStatus.
  M: Background-refresh mechanics are missing: RequestBackgroundRefresh spawns a separate thread (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:865-895) that runs RefreshView/BuildSnapshot (817-863), creates new transports (670-740), and retires removed ones (852-858). This is what decides whether transports (and thus capability clamps) are rebuilt; P2 step 5 mentions only the RequestBackgroundRefresh() call.
  M: Origin of mrKey/tokenId is missing: the token copied into entry.mrKey at submit (kv_semantics/src/client/kv_client_impl.cc:451-459) is produced by RegisterRegionsOnce via TransProvider::RegisterMemory/GetMemTokenId and stored in registeredRegions_ (kv_client_impl.cc:357-390, 422-424). Without this the BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED branch and the 'caller-supplied mrKey discarded' step lack their prerequisite.
  M: Send-only bookkeeping is missing: sendCompletedAt, sendReturned, the send metrics and task->onSendComplete() run only in the status.ok() && !ioBatches.empty() branch (kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:328-350). On any pre-send failure the client's remainingTransportSendTasks is never decremented and kv_client_task_send_duration_seconds is never recorded. P7 step 5 presents these actions unconditionally.
  M: PrepareTaskSubBatches' first-failure early return is missing: on the first failing slice it returns immediately (kv_semantics/src/trans/task/kv_submit_flow.cc:66-79), so later slices are never built; Execute then runs AssignSubBatchConnections on the partial context vector (trans_task_executor.cc:320) and AbortSubBatchesBeforeSend (363, 227-246). P5 step 1 says only 'one TransportSubBatchContext per slice, built sequentially'.
  M: The transport Cancel path is missing: TransportTaskExecutor::Cancel fills CANCELED into task.entryStatus and every not-yet-completed sub-batch, sets sub-batch status/state, releases resources and completes the task (kv_semantics/src/trans/task/trans_task_executor.cc:258-277). errorPaths mentions cancellation only generically (Shutdown drain).

  M: Unpublished-but-built snapshots: RefreshView calls BuildSnapshot at 843 then re-checks ShouldPublishView under mutex_ (846-851) and may return OK without publishing. Providers created/bound during BuildTransport (732-737) stay in providerMemoryStates_ even though nextSnapshot is discarded; the trace only says 'the new snapshot is not published'. P12/step5 only describes the Poll-based path.
  M: Multi-sub-batch abort semantics: Execute fills the entire task.entryStatus with CANCELED (trans_task_executor.cc:232) and only already-built sub-batches override it; PrepareTaskSubBatches returns on the first failing sub-batch (kv_submit_flow.cc:69-78), so entries in later sub-batches surface CANCELED, not BUFFER_NOT_REGISTERED. TransportTaskManager::BuildResult copies entry status only from subBatchContexts (trans_task_manager.cc:86-104).

Handle 0 would then be keyed in registeredRegions_, skipped by UnregisterRegions (kv_client_impl.cc:581), and would match a KVBuffer whose buffer.handle is left at its default kInvalidMRHandle (kv_types.h:119) during the SubmitAsync lookup (kv_client_impl.cc:456-457).

Nothing validates that an entry's buffer region matches the region registered under its handle.
  M: Shutdown bypasses UnregisterRegions: it clears registeredRegions_ at kv_client_impl.cc:216 and unregisters each ProviderMemoryState directly in reverse (231-240), so Shutdown does not apply the 'still mapped?' filtering of 610-619.

CORRECTIONS:
  C: Canonical/handle provenance: mrHandles are memoryProvider_'s handles (kv_client_impl.cc:357-358) stored as RegisteredMemory.handle (387-390, 423). providerMemoryStates_[0] is memoryProvider_ (Init 162-166) and its regionHandles is an identity map canonical->canonical (395-397 + 414-421); states appended by BuildTransport (732-737) map canonical->provider-local handle. Hence the {30,20,10,10} order (tests 1602-1616): t30, t20, t10, then memoryProvider_.
  C: P4/step3 correction: BindProviderRegions clears localHandles on entry (kv_client_impl.cc:746) and is passed the out-vector registeredRegions, not the member registeredRegions_ map; token equality is enforced against registeredRegions[index].tokenId (772-782), which is what makes a memoryProvider_-issued token valid on the other providers.
  C: P3/step3: RegisterMemoryDesc.localAddr defaults to 0 and is never set at registration (trans_provider.h:51-56); BindProviderRegions likewise omits it (752-761). deviceId/numaNode are not forwarded (kv_client_impl.cc:349-355).
  C: P13/step1: ASU order is sorted by NodeId with kInvalidNodeId skipped (GetSortedAsuIds at kv_client_impl.cc:920-931); reuse is by nodeId from oldSnapshot (638-641).   C: P13/step2: mutex_ and memoryMu_ are not nested. The config copy is taken in a mutex_ scope (674-682), released, then memoryMu_ is taken (685); the real contention is that memoryMu_ is held across nextTransport->Init (710) and BindProviderRegions (725). Note config_.sharedProviderMode is read at 687 under memoryMu_ while config_ is written under mutex_ (Shutdown 205-211).
  C: P12/step3: BuildEntrySubBatchRequest is defined at trans_task_executor.cc:351; it assigns entryStatus (356), calls ValidateSqeMrKeys (359-363) and marks the sub-batch COMPLETED with the error via SetSubBatchBuildFailed (sqe_request.cc:101-106). The call site is kv_submit_flow.cc:69-70, and the error propagates out of PrepareTaskSubBatches at kv_submit_flow.cc:77.
  C: P10/step3: mrKey is RegisteredMemory.tokenId (kv_client_impl.cc:457), not any per-provider value. The trace correctly notes the caller value is discarded (455); worth adding that a NO-entry (mrKey nullopt) is only rejected in the transport entry-batch path (ValidateSqeMrKeys, sqe_request.cc:45-55) and never for query/delete.
  C: P8/step3 refinement: UnregisterProviderRegions aggregates per-handle statuses (801-814). When it fails, some local handles may already be unregistered while the provider's whole canonical->local map is preserved (597-603), so a retry re-sends handles that may no longer exist; this is the documented asymmetry and is exercised by the failUnregister path (tests 1585-1600).
  C: P6/step1: providerHandles is vector<vector<MRHandle>> sized to providerMemoryStates_ (392); the commit loop indexes providerHandles[providerIndex][regionIndex] for every state, which is safe only because a failing bind returns before the commit (399-412).
  C: P1/step1: RegisterRegionsOnce is reachable only through RegisterRegions in this file (only caller at 325); needRefresh is caller-initialized false (324) and is used solely for the post-call RequestBackgroundRefresh (326).

ERRORS:
